Abstract: Deep research (DR) systems are increasingly used for complex information-seeking tasks, but existing works mainly focus on generating reports and summaries. In contrast, many enterprise tasks instead require an agent to identify concrete workflows which is a sequence of action-steps. For example, rather than summarizing budgeting policies, an agent should be able to determine the steps needed to answer a question such as: "How do I request new headcount given a fixed budget?". Therefore, we introduce DRFLOW, a benchmark for evaluating personalized workflows predicted by agents from heterogeneous sources. Each task requires the agent to identify relevant evidence from scattered sources, then use that evidence to predict the correct action-step sequence for the user's task. DRFLOW contains 100 tasks across five domains, with 1,246 reference workflow steps grounded in more than 3,900 sources. We define seven diagnostic metrics covering factual grounding, step recovery, structural ordering, condition resolution, and personalization. We further present DRFLOW-Agent (DRFA), a workflow-oriented reference agent to predict personalized workflow. We show that although DRFA improves over strong baseline agents (upto 10.02% average F1 score), there is substantial room for improvement remains across these workflow metrics, indicating that predicting complete and correct personalized workflows remains a challenging frontier for deep research.

</details>


### [111] [The Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset: Reconstructing U.S. Corporate and Financial Disclosures into Layout-Faithful and Token-Efficient Pretraining Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18192)
*Nick Bettencourt,Xiaowei Ding,Kay Giesecke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SEFD是一个从SEC文件重建的开源长上下文金融数据集（152B tokens），将审计财务报表等文件转化为MultiMarkdown格式，具有低于0.1%的Common Crawl重叠率，并附带两个金融推理基准（EDGAR-Forecast和EDGAR-OCR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量长上下文公开网络语料日益枯竭，现有长上下文金融语料要么专有且昂贵，要么是合成生成的，或者集中在编程等狭窄领域，缺乏真实、高质量的长上下文金融数据用于LLM预训练和评估。

Method: 将SEC文件重建为布局保真的MultiMarkdown格式，构建152B tokens的SEFD-v1快照（更大档案库估计550B tokens），并在此基础上开发两个基准：EDGAR-Forecast（文件锚定的数值预测）和EDGAR-OCR（金融表格转录）。

Result: SEFD-v1初始公开快照包含152B tokens，更大的18.5M文件档案库估计550B tokens，与Common Crawl衍生语料的重叠率低于0.1%。

Conclusion: SEFD提供了开源、高质量、Token高效的长上下文金融语料，能够作为金融推理、预测、合规和文档理解的基础，弥补了现有金融长上下文数据的缺失。

Abstract: As high-quality public web corpora become increasingly exhausted, clean long-context documents have become a scarce and expensive source of training data for large language models (LLMs). Existing long-context corpora are often proprietary and costly to acquire, synthetically generated, or concentrated in narrow domains such as programming. We introduce the Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset (SEFD), an open reconstruction of SEC filings into layout-faithful MultiMarkdown for financial language modeling and evaluation. SEFD makes audited financial statements, risk disclosures, ownership reports, accounting notes, and market-moving event filings usable as long-context pretraining data and as a basis for financial reasoning, forecasting, compliance, and document understanding. The resulting corpus is token-efficient, model-ready, and has less than 0.1% overlap with Common Crawl-derived corpora. We release SEFD-v1, a 152B-token initial public snapshot, and provide corpus-level analyses of a larger 18.5M-filing archive estimated at 550B tokens. We further introduce two SEFD-derived benchmarks: EDGAR-Forecast, which evaluates filing-grounded numerical forecasting after model knowledge cutoffs, and EDGAR-OCR, which evaluates transcription of complex financial tables.

</details>


### [112] [Fixed-Point Reasoners: Stable and Adaptive Deep Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18206)
*Sajad Movahedi,Vera Milovanović,Shlomo Libo Feigin,Alexander Theus,Thomas Hofmann,Valentina Boeva,T. Konstantin Rusch,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对循环架构中深度增加导致的信号传播问题，本文采用预层归一化和残差缩放，提出基于Transformer的定点推理模型FPRM，利用定点收敛作为循环架构的端到端停机制，使模型能够根据任务难度自适应调整计算量，在Sudoku、Maze、状态追踪和ARC-AGI等推理基准上表现有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环架构在组合推理任务中具有学习逐步过程的归纳偏置，但随着循环层数增加（即推迟停止决策），会出现类似深度架构的信号传播问题，影响模型解的质量。

Method: 采用预层归一化和残差缩放缓解信号传播问题，提出FPRM模型，在循环架构中使用定点收敛作为端到端的停机制。

Result: FPRM能够根据任务难度自适应调整计算量，在Sudoku、Maze、状态追踪和ARC-AGI等推理基准上表现有效。

Conclusion: 定点停机制使循环架构在推理任务中既能适应计算需求，又能取得良好性能。

Abstract: Looped architectures provide an inductive bias toward learning step-by-step procedures for tasks that require compositional reasoning. The number of effective layers reached by looping determines the quality of the solution these models find. Like deep architectures, looped architectures are prone to a signal propagation problem induced by depth as the halting decision is postponed. In this paper, we address this signal propagation issue using pre-norm layers and residual scaling. Building on these architectural modifications, we propose FPRM, a Transformer-based Fixed-Point Reasoning Model that uses fixed-point convergence as an end-to-end halting mechanism in a looped architecture. We show that fixed-point halting allows FPRM to adapt its compute to task difficulty. FPRM is effective on common reasoning benchmarks, namely Sudoku, Maze, state-tracking, and ARC-AGI.

</details>


### [113] [EvolveNav: Proactive Preflection and Self-Evolving Memory for Zero-Shot Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18235)
*Qi Chai,Wenhao Shen,Nanjie Yao,Yue Xia,Kaiyong Zhao,Jie Ma,Guosheng Lin,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自我进化的零样本目标导航框架，通过构建智能规则记忆、基于置信上界的检索策略以及记忆引导的预反思模块，实现了测试时的持续改进，在成功率和探索效率上均优于现有零样本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本目标导航方法依赖静态先验知识，缺乏适应性，导致重复错误和高代价的试错过程。

Method: 1) 构建智能规则记忆，从历史轨迹中提取可操作的规则知识；2) 提出基于置信上界（UCB）的检索策略，平衡语义相关性和历史成功率来筛选有效规则；3) 引入记忆引导的预反思模块，在行动前预测潜在结果，减少低效探索。

Result: 在多个基准上超越现有零样本方法，成功率提升10.1%，同时减少了不必要的探索步骤。

Conclusion: 通过自我进化的记忆机制，实现了零样本目标导航的测试时持续改进，有效提升了导航成功率与探索效率。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Object-Goal Navigation (ZS-OGN) requires embodied agents to explore and locate target objects without any prior training. To this end, recent methods leverage foundation models. But they typically rely on static priors and lack adaptation, which leads to repeated errors and costly trial and error. In this paper, we propose a self-evolving ZS-OGN framework that enables continuous test-time improvement. Specifically, we build an agentic rule memory by extracting actionable knowledge from past trajectories. Then, we propose a retrieval strategy based on upper confidence bound, selecting effective rules by balancing semantic relevance and historical success. In addition, we introduce a memory-guided preflection module that forecasts potential outcomes before action, reducing inefficient exploration. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms existing zero-shot baselines, achieving a 10.1\% improvement in success rate with fewer unnecessary steps.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [On the Strong Duality in Continuous-time and Discrete-time Linear Quadratic Regulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17208)
*Yuto Watanabe,Yang Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了连续时间和离散时间系统LQR问题的强对偶性，通过线性算子Ψ建立统一的非凸LQR框架，推导拉格朗日对偶问题，并利用秩约束的SDP松弛证明强对偶性，最终恢复经典代数Riccati方程和最优反馈增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视LQR问题中的强对偶性，探索其与典型假设及原始-对偶解唯一性之间的内在联系，并揭示连续时间与离散时间系统之间的结构对称性。

Method: 通过线性算子Ψ构建统一的非凸LQR问题框架，推导拉格朗日对偶问题，利用秩约束的紧SDP松弛证明强对偶性，并利用线性算子及其伴随算子的性质进行对偶性分析。

Result: 建立了连续时间和离散时间LQR问题的强对偶性；证明了SDP松弛（去除秩约束后）的原始-对偶最优解能够以构造性方式恢复经典代数Riccati方程和最优反馈增益。

Conclusion: 强对偶性的建立仅依赖于温和的标准假设，且两个时间域之间存在结构对称性。

Abstract: This paper revisits the strong duality in the linear quadratic regulator (LQR) for continuous-time and discrete-time systems, and explores its interconnection with typical assumptions and the uniqueness of primal-dual solutions. Using a linear operator $Ψ$, we formulate a common nonconvex LQR problem that captures both time domains. We then derive its Lagrange dual problem and establish the strong duality via a rank-constrained tight semidefinite program (SDP) relaxation. Further, we show that the primal-dual optimal solutions to the SDP relaxation, after dropping the rank constraint, recover the classical algebraic Riccati equations and optimal feedback gains in a constructive manner. The dual derivation and strong duality analysis rely on mild standard assumptions and exploit the properties of the linear operator and its adjoint, revealing a structural symmetry between the two time domains.

</details>


### [115] [Accelerated Convex Optimization via Hamiltonian Dynamics with Deterministic Integration Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17260)
*Xiuyuan Wang,Vishwak Srinivasan,Qiang Fu,Siddharth Mitra,Ashia Wilson,Andre Wibisono*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于哈密顿动力学的平滑凸优化算法，通过利用平均哈密顿流轨迹的收缩性而非轨迹终点的收缩性，实现了确定性的加速收敛保证，并推导了具有最优一阶复杂度的离散时间实现，将哈密顿动力学确立为确定性加速凸优化的有效算法原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于哈密顿动力学的优化方法要么局限于二次目标函数，要么仅能在期望意义下保证加速收敛，缺乏确定性的加速收敛保证。本文旨在克服这些限制，建立哈密顿动力学在确定性加速凸优化中的通用理论框架。

Method: 利用平均哈密顿流轨迹的收缩性而非轨迹终点的收缩性进行分析；先分析理想化的连续时间算法，再推导实用的离散时间实现，并确保其具有最优的一阶复杂度。

Result: 所提出的哈密顿动力学优化方法在平滑凸优化中实现了确定性的加速收敛，离散时间实现达到了最优的一阶复杂度。

Conclusion: 哈密顿动力学可以被视为确定性加速凸优化的一个有用算法原语，为设计具有理论保证的高效优化算法提供了新途径。

Abstract: We develop Hamiltonian dynamics-based algorithms for smooth convex optimization that achieve accelerated rates of convergence. By exploiting contraction of averaged Hamiltonian flow trajectories rather than requiring contraction at trajectory endpoints, we show that Hamiltonian dynamics-based optimization methods admit deterministic and accelerated convergence guarantees, extending prior work that is limited to quadratic objectives or holds only in expectation. We analyze an idealized continuous-time algorithm and derive practical discrete-time implementations with optimal first-order complexity, thereby establishing Hamiltonian dynamics as a useful algorithmic primitive for deterministic accelerated convex optimization.

</details>


### [116] [Adaptive Proximal Methods for Weakly Convex Optimization with Unknown Parameter: Deterministic and Stochastic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17285)
*Miaolan Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对非光滑、非凸的ρ-弱凸函数优化问题，提出自适应近端引导方案(APS)，在未知弱凸参数ρ的情况下，通过双向自适应调整近端参数，实现了确定性和随机环境下O(ε⁻²)的迭代复杂度，且对随机噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多学习和信号恢复中的非光滑、非凸目标函数具有ρ-弱凸性，但实际中弱凸参数ρ往往未知，现有方法通常需要预先知道ρ或采用保守的固定参数，导致效率低下。本文旨在设计一种无需知晓ρ、能自适应调整近端参数的一阶优化算法。

Method: 提出自适应近端引导方案(APS)，采用单次尝试(trial)的近端算法，通过下降测试(descent test)在线双向调整近端参数，使其能够利用局部有利结构。算法不要求目标函数全局Lipschitz连续或光滑。

Result: 确定环境下，APS达到O(ε⁻²)迭代复杂度以产生ε-次梯度稳定点。随机环境下，APS以高概率达到O(ε⁻²)迭代界使Moreau包络梯度低于ε，且允许函数差分估计有偏和重尾，随机近端或acles仅需在近端参数低于1/(2ρ)时以常概率足够精确。

Conclusion: APS是一种无需知晓ρ的、对噪声鲁棒的自适应近端算法，在弱凸优化问题上具有理论保证的迭代复杂度，适用于学习和信号恢复等实际场景。

Abstract: Many nonsmooth, nonconvex objectives in learning and signal recovery are $ρ$-weakly convex. We minimize such a function in deterministic and stochastic settings when the weak-convexity parameter $ρ$ is unknown. The objective is not required to be globally Lipschitz continuous or smooth. We propose the Adaptive Prox-Guided Scheme (APS), a one-trial proximal algorithm that adapts the proximal parameter online and bidirectionally through a descent test, allowing it to exploit favorable local structure.
  In the deterministic setting, APS obtains an $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iteration complexity for producing an $\varepsilon$-subgradient stationary point. In the stochastic setting, APS achieves a high-probability $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iteration bound for driving the Moreau-envelope gradient below $\varepsilon$. This result holds under deliberately weak oracle assumptions: the function-difference estimates may be biased and heavy-tailed, and the stochastic proximal oracle need only be sufficiently accurate with constant probability when the proximal parameter lies below $1/(2ρ)$ (unknown to the algorithm), and can be arbitrary otherwise.

</details>


### [117] [Coarse Preference Reporting in the Bottleneck Model: Approximate Strategyproofness and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17400)
*Takara Sakai,Riki Kawase*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对瓶颈动态系统最优（DSO）调度问题，设计了一种基于离散时隙的粗粒度报告机制。车辆从有限时隙菜单中选择，运营商依据报告实现DSO分配并收取容量影子价格作为通行费。理论证明最坏谎报收益和期望效率损失均随时隙宽度二次衰减，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VCG机制虽能实现策略证明性，但规则复杂且计算负担重。现实中预约和调度系统（如自动车辆管理车道、机场时隙分配、配送预约窗口）通常采用离散时隙的粗粒度报告接口，因此需要设计在粗粒度报告下仍能实现良好性能的DSO机制。

Method: 设计基于离散时隙的DSO机制：车辆从有限时隙菜单中选择，运营商根据报告实现DSO分配并收取容量影子价格作为通行费。通过理论分析推导最坏谎报收益和期望效率损失随时的衰减速率，并进行数值实验验证。

Result: 最坏谎报收益和期望效率损失均随着时隙宽度减小而二次衰减。效率损失在容量约束下成立，而最坏谎报收益需额外条件（偏好时间分布和调度成本函数）。无通行费时，即使时隙无限细化，谎报激励仍然存在，说明通行费还起到诱导真实报告的作用。

Conclusion: 基于粗粒度离散时隙的DSO机制在实用性和理论性能之间取得了良好平衡，时隙宽度可控制效率损失和谎报激励，通行费是激励真实报告的关键要素。

Abstract: A central operator schedules each vehicle's passage time through a bottleneck to achieve a dynamic system optimum (DSO). The assignment depends on each vehicle's preferred arrival time, which is private and must be elicited from each vehicle. Mechanisms that elicit exact preferences, such as the Vickrey-Clarke-Groves (VCG) mechanism, can achieve strategyproofness but involve relatively complex rules and a computational burden on the operator. We focus instead on coarse reporting, in which each vehicle selects from a finite menu of time slots of a common width. This discrete interface already structures reservation and appointment systems in practice, including managed lanes for automated vehicles, airport slot allocation, and delivery appointment windows. We design a slot-based DSO mechanism on this coarse interface, in which the operator implements DSO assignment based on the reported slots and charges a capacity shadow price as a toll, and evaluate its performance. We prove that both the worst-case misreporting gain and the expected efficiency loss decrease quadratically in the slot width. The efficiency loss decays in this way under binding capacity, while the worst-case misreporting gain requires an additional condition on the preferred arrival time distribution and the schedule cost function. Analyzing the no-toll case, we find that the misreporting incentive persists, however finely the slots are refined, indicating that the toll also serves to elicit truthful reports. Numerical experiments support these theoretical results and show that they continue to hold in parameter regions outside the sufficient conditions.

</details>


### [118] [Contextual Robust Optimization for AI Data Center Scheduling with Statistical Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17466)
*Yijie Yang,Xi Weng,Yue Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对AI数据中心运营中可再生能源发电和AI工作负载的预测误差问题，提出一种基于情境鲁棒优化的框架，通过损失驱动的不确定性学习模型和联合机会约束调度方法，在保证可靠可行性的同时降低运营成本5.57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工作负载快速增长导致数据中心电力需求和碳排放显著增加，现有碳感知调度方法难以有效处理可再生能源发电和AI工作负载的预测误差，以及训练与推理工作负载在计算特性上的异构性。

Method: 提出情境鲁棒优化框架，包含：(1) 显式建模AI训练和推理工作负载的异构计算特性；(2) 基于损失的底层不确定性学习模型，将情境特征直接映射为协变量相关的不确定集；(3) 将情境联合机会约束调度问题重构为可处理的鲁棒优化问题；(4) 开发校准算法提供有限样本概率可行性保证。

Result: 基于真实AI工作负载轨迹和可再生能源发电数据的数值实验表明，所提方法相比基准方法平均降低运营成本5.57%，同时保持可靠的可行性和较强的计算可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的情境鲁棒优化框架能有效应对AI数据中心运营中的多重不确定性，兼顾经济性、可行性保障和计算效率，为低碳AI数据中心调度提供可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI workloads is substantially increasing data center electricity demand and carbon emissions, motivating the development of carbon-aware scheduling methods. However, effective scheduling is challenging because renewable generation and AI workloads are subject to forecast errors, while training and inference workloads exhibit heterogeneity in computational characteristics. This paper proposes a contextual robust optimization framework for AI data center operation. The proposed model explicitly captures the heterogeneous computational characteristics of AI training and inference workloads. To deal with renewable generation and workload forecast errors, we develop loss-based uncertainty learning models that directly map contextual features to covariate-dependent uncertainty sets. The resulting contextual joint chance-constrained scheduling problem is reformulated into a tractable robust optimization problem, and a calibration algorithm is developed to provide finite-sample probabilistic feasibility guarantees for multiple joint chance constraints. Numerical experiments based on real-world AI workload traces and renewable generation data show that the proposed method reduces operating costs by an average of 5.57% compared to benchmark methods while maintaining reliable feasibility and strong computational scalability.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond IGO-Flow: Toward Convergence Analysis of IGO in Continuous Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17523)
*Ryosuke Kimura,Youhei Akimoto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散时间信息几何优化(IGO)在连续空间中的收敛性，证明了在强凸二次目标函数上，多变量高斯族的IGO算法能使协方差矩阵收敛到零矩阵，且均值向量在适当条件下收敛到全局最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IGO为黑盒优化提供了统一框架，但其收敛理论大多局限于连续时间理想化模型（如IGO流），缺乏离散时间更新下的收敛性分析，理论与实践之间存在差距。

Method: 在指数族的期望参数坐标下将离散时间IGO建模为自然梯度更新，针对多变量高斯族和强凸二次目标函数，同时考虑全协方差自适应、固定正学习率和基于分位数的权重。

Result: 证明了协方差矩阵收敛到零矩阵；在适当缩放后的协方差矩阵条件数在足够频繁的迭代中有界时，均值向量收敛到全局最优解。

Conclusion: 推进了IGO的收敛理论，弥合了IGO数学理论与实际协方差自适应搜索方法（如CMA-ES）之间的差距。

Abstract: Information-Geometric Optimization (IGO) provides a unified framework for black-box optimization by interpreting the adaptation of a search distribution as a natural gradient update. Despite its conceptual importance, the convergence theory of IGO remains limited: most existing results concern continuous-time idealizations such as the IGO flow, rather than discrete-time updates with non-infinitesimal learning rates. In this paper, we study discrete-time IGO in continuous spaces, formulated as natural gradient updates in the expectation-parameter coordinates of an exponential family. In particular, we analyze IGO over the multivariate Gaussian family on strongly convex quadratic objective functions. Our analysis covers a setting that simultaneously incorporates full covariance adaptation, a fixed positive learning rate, and quantile-based weights. In this setting, we prove that the covariance matrix converges to the zero matrix. We further show that the mean vector converges to the global optimum, provided that the condition number of the appropriately scaled covariance matrix is bounded at sufficiently frequent iterations. These results advance the convergence theory of IGO and help bridge the gap between the mathematical theory of IGO and practical covariance-adaptive search methods such as CMA-ES.

</details>


### [120] [An Adaptive Algorithm for the Approximation of General Linear-Parametric Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17552)
*Levin Nemesch,Stefan Ruzika,Clemens Thielen,Alina Wittmann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对线性多参数优化问题，提出了一种新的自适应近似（及精确）算法，突破了现有算法不允许负参数依赖且参数集固定在正象限的限制，可应用于更广泛的参数优化问题。同时证明了对于线性多参数最大化问题，参数集上非负最优目标值的假设不足以确保可近似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性多参数优化问题的近似算法共享一组共同的假设——不允许负参数依赖且参数集固定在正象限，这严重限制了可近似参数优化问题的类别。本文旨在克服这些限制，提出适用于更广泛问题的算法。

Method: 提出一种新的自适应近似（及精确）算法，该算法建立在参数优化和多目标优化两个领域的现有算法基础上，对其进行了推广。同时提供了参数集变换的结构性结果。

Result: 新算法适用于更广泛的线性多参数优化问题类别，突破了现有算法对负参数依赖和正象限参数集的限制。此外，证明了对于线性多参数最大化问题，参数集上非负最优目标值的假设不足以确保可近似性。

Conclusion: 本文拓展了线性多参数优化问题可近似性的边界，为更广泛的实际应用提供了理论基础和算法支持。

Abstract: Linear-multi-parametric optimization problems are a widely studied class of optimization problems. The objective function in such a problem is affine linear dependent on a parameter vector, and the goal is to compute a set of solutions that contains an optimal solution for every fixed parameter vector. However, this is known to be computationally challenging: The underlying non-parametric problem might be NP-hard, and, in addition, optimal solution sets might have exponential cardinality. Parametric approximation aims at providing polynomial-time algorithms that overcome these challenges. Instead of computing an optimal solution set, the goal is to compute an approximation set that contains only an approximate solution for every fixed parameter vector. Several new parametric approximation algorithms have been developed in recent literature. However, all of these share a common set of assumptions, which limits the class of parametric optimization problems that can be approximated. Namely, they do not allow negative parameter dependencies and have their parameter sets fixed to the positive orthant. We present a new adaptive approximation (and, also, exact) algorithm that can be applied to a wider class of linear-multi-parametric optimization problems. Our algorithm builds upon existing algorithms from both the fields of parametric and multi-objective optimization and generalizes these algorithms. In addition, we provide structural results for the transformation of parameter sets, and demonstrate that, for linear-multi-parametric maximization problems, the assumption of non-negative optimal objective values over the whole parameter set is not sufficient to ensure approximability.

</details>


### [121] [Two-Echelon Delivery Vehicle Sharing and Repositioning in Hyperconnected Urban Logistic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17608)
*Xiaoyue Liu,Benoit Montreuil*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种超连接城市物流系统中基于双梯队车辆共享与重定位的集装箱化配送模型，通过物理互联网三级枢纽网络和分解启发式算法，实现成本降低16.8%和碳排放减少45.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足可持续高效的城市配送需求，解决传统单梯队配送效率低、碳排放高的问题，利用物理互联网理念实现开放资源共享和动态车辆重定位。

Method: 构建多周期整数规划模型，集成基于路径的服务车辆规划与基于弧的集装箱路由；提出基于分解的启发式算法，结合容量感知流分配，按梯队和区域划分子问题。

Result: 以亚特兰大都市区为例，双梯队超连接配送系统在全市场份额下较传统单梯队方案减少CO2-eq排放45.0%，降低总成本16.8%；车辆重定位进一步使成本降低17.7%。

Conclusion: 所提出的双梯队车辆共享与重定位模型可显著提升城市物流的可持续性和经济性，验证了物理互联网理念在超连接物流系统中的应用价值。

Abstract: In response to the growing demand for sustainable and efficient urban deliveries, this study introduces a two-echelon vehicle sharing and repositioning problem for containerized delivery operations within a hyperconnected urban logistics system. We leverage a Physical Internet (PI)-enabled three-tier logistic hub network, comprising gateway, local, and access hubs, to facilitate efficient flows. By adopting containerized delivery, vehicles can rapidly swap standardized modular containers at hubs to reduce handling time. Moreover, inspired by the PI concept of open resource sharing, we determine optimal service routes within a two-echelon structure that jointly utilizes heterogeneous vehicle fleets and enables dynamic vehicle relocation across hubs. We formulate this problem as a multi-period integer program that integrates path-based service vehicle planning with arc-based container routing. To address real-world large-scale instances, we propose a decomposition-based heuristic with capacity-aware flow assignment, which partitions the problem into subproblems structured by echelons and regions. A case study on the Atlanta metropolitan area demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed model and solution approach. Experimental results show that the two-echelon hyperconnected delivery system reduces CO2-eq emissions by 45.0% and total costs by 16.8% at full market share compared to a traditional single-echelon alternative, while enabling vehicle repositioning further lowers costs by up to 17.7%.

</details>


### [122] [A Projection-Free Algorithm for Variational Inequalities in Hilbert Spaces with Strong Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17640)
*Reinier Diaz Millan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种求解实Hilbert空间中点对集极大单调算子变分不等式的新投影方法，该方法仅使用半空间交投影，在Slater修正条件下实现强收敛，并给出了显式、近似和有限候选oracle实现，在非光滑和大规模约束问题上展示了数值效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解带凸不等式约束变分不等式的方法往往需要计算到约束集C的度量投影，当C复杂或非光滑时代价高昂；此外，针对点对集极大单调算子的强收敛算法尚不完善。本文旨在设计一种仅依赖半空间投影、避免度量投影、且能处理不可微约束的强收敛算法。

Method: 提出一种投影方法，核心包括：(1) 使用次梯度割处理可行性，当试验算子点不可行时基于Slater严格可行点进行修正；(2) 通过Minty型分离半空间表示变分不等式，在算子可行图点处生成；(3) 引入Haugazeau半空间以实现最佳逼近收敛；(4) 在Slater修正的可行分离条件下，设计了显式、近似和有限候选oracle三种实现方式。

Result: 证明了全序列强收敛到初始点在解集上的投影；得到了最优迭代O(N^{-1/2})的残差估计（包括步残差、可行性违反和Minty间隙）；在多点对集强单调情形下推导了收敛率推论；在多种非光滑和大规模约束问题（包括凸二次极大、离散最优控制、混合范数稀疏恢复、Cournot-Nash容量均衡和ℓ1-次微分）上进行了数值验证。

Conclusion: 所提方法在点对集极大单调算子变分不等式上实现了避免度量投影的强收敛，具有良好的理论保证（全序列强收敛+O(N^{-1/2})残差估计）和广泛的数值适用性。

Abstract: We study variational inequalities governed by a point-to-set maximal monotone operator in a real Hilbert space and constrained by a convex inequality \(C=\{x\in\Hi:c(x)\le0\}\), where the defining function \(c\) is continuous and not necessarily differentiable. The proposed method uses only projections onto intersections of half-spaces and avoids the metric projection onto \(C\). Feasibility is handled by subgradient cuts and, when a trial operator point is infeasible, by a Slater correction based on a fixed strictly feasible point. The variational inequality is represented by Minty-type separating half-spaces generated at feasible graph points of the operator, and a Haugazeau half-space is added to obtain best-approximation convergence. Under a Slater-corrected feasible-separation condition, together with explicit exact, approximate and finite-candidate oracle realisations, the whole sequence converges strongly to \(P_{S^*}(x^0)\), the projection of the initial point onto the solution set. We also derive best-iterate \(O(N^{-1/2})\) residual estimates for the step residual, feasibility violation and Minty gap. The analysis is stated directly for point-to-set maximal monotone operators, while the concrete oracle realisations include finite-dimensional single-valued models. We record the consequences of strong monotonicity in the point-to-set setting and provide numerical comparisons on nonsmooth and large-scale constraints, including maxima of convex quadratics, a discretised optimal-control problem, mixed-norm sparse recovery, a Cournot--Nash capacity equilibrium, and a genuine point-to-set \(\ell_1\)-subdifferential example.

</details>


### [123] [Robust Bayesian Portfolio Optimization with Discrepancy-based Posterior Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17643)
*Zongxia Liang,Yang Liu,Xingjian Ma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了漂移不确定下连续时间鲁棒贝叶斯投资组合优化问题，提出基于可观测状态的反馈型模糊框架，推导出修正的HJBI方程，并在指数效用下得到半显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯学习过程中后验估计存在不确定性，传统的鲁棒优化方法会导致时间不一致性问题，需要新的框架来解决。

Method: 采用基于散度的模糊集（Wasserstein和L^p距离）描述后验不确定性，引入反馈型模糊框架将模糊条件化于可观测状态，推导修正的HJBI方程；在指数效用下简化为半线性抛物型PDE并通过验证定理证明经典解存在性。

Result: 建立了反馈型模糊框架下的修正HJBI方程，在指数效用情形下得到了半显式解并验证了经典解的存在性。

Conclusion: 所提出的反馈型模糊框架有效解决了时间不一致性，为漂移不确定下的鲁棒贝叶斯投资组合优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time robust Bayesian portfolio optimization problem under drift uncertainty of risky assets. The investor learns unknown asset drifts through Bayesian filtering while considering uncertainty around posterior estimates via discrepancy-based ambiguity sets, including Wasserstein and $L^p$ distances. To address the resulting time inconsistency, we introduce a feedback-type ambiguity framework that reformulates ambiguity conditionally on observable states. This leads to a modified Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman--Isaacs (HJBI) equation characterizing the value function and the optimal strategy. For a semi-explicit solution example, we use the exponential utility to derive a reduced semilinear parabolic PDE and establish existence of classical solutions via a verification theorem.

</details>


### [124] [Gray-Box Optimization using Optimism in the Face of Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17726)
*Katrin Baumgärtner,Léo Simpson,Moritz Diehl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用已知问题结构（损失函数和先验参数集）的序列灰箱优化方法，基于乐观面对不确定性原则最小化置信下界，在噪声观测下迭代估计未知模型参数，并通过理论 regret 分析和数值实验证明了该方法优于结构无关方法，在线性随机 bandit 特例上改进了现有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标函数由损失函数和参数化模型复合而成，模型参数未知且需从噪声观测中迭代估计。该问题泛化了参数化黑箱优化（如上下文随机线性 bandit），现有方法未能充分利用已知的结构信息。

Method: 提出一种结构利用方法，基于乐观面对不确定性原则，通过最小化真实目标函数的置信下界来平衡探索与利用；利用已知的损失函数和先验参数集，并结合多输出线性最小二乘估计中参数置信集的最新界来改进 regret 分析。

Result: 提供了详细的 regret 分析，在随机线性 bandit 特例上由于使用了最新的参数置信集界而改进了现有最优结果；数值实验表明结构利用方法显著优于结构无关方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的灰箱优化方法通过利用已知问题结构能够在理论上获得更优的 regret 界，实践中也表现出更好的性能，验证了结构信息在序贯优化中的价值。

Abstract: This paper considers sequential gray-box optimization where the objective function is given as the composition of a loss function and a parametric model. Crucially, the parameters of the model are unknown and need to be iteratively estimated from noisy observations of the model outputs. This problem setup generalizes the parametric black-box optimization problem known as (contextual) stochastic linear bandit. To address the sequential gray-box optimization problem, we propose a structure-exploiting method that leverages known problem structure given in terms of the loss function and an a priori set of admissible parameters. The method is based on the principle of optimism in the face of uncertainty and trades off exploration and exploitation by minimizing a lower confidence bound on the true objective function. We provide a detailed regret analysis of the novel method, improving on state-of-the-art results for the special case of linear stochastic bandits due to the use of a recently published bound for the parameter confidence sets arising in multi-output linear least-squares estimation. Numerical examples illustrate the superior performance of structure-exploiting methods compared to structure-agnostic approaches.

</details>


### [125] [Symplectic Transversality and Endpoint Green Estimates for Finite-Horizon Pontryagin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17762)
*Pyuyi Chufeng Huang,Zikang Song,Xingshu Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域离散时间庞特里亚金边界值系统在光滑控制消除后的水平一致局部分支，通过两点端点逆线性化、缩放稳定-不稳定边界横截性验证、端点修正格林估计及加权压缩，建立了存在性、唯一性、Lipschitz依赖性和一阶展开，并证明了辛和Riccati准则可验证逆假设，涵盖所有具有可逆动力学的可镇定线性二次系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论在处理有限时域离散时间庞特里亚金边界值系统时，缺乏对水平一致局部分支的系统性分析，尤其是对于非线性终点映射和耦合数据情形下的存在性、唯一性及参数依赖性的统一框架。

Method: 利用两点端点逆线性化作为核心输入，通过缩放稳定-不稳定边界横截性验证该逆的存在性；建立端点修正格林估计；结合加权压缩论证推导存在性、唯一性、Lipschitz依赖性和一阶展开，其中常数与水平无关。通过辛和Riccati准则在矩阵数据层面验证逆假设。

Result: 证明了在光滑控制消除后，有限时域离散时间庞特里亚金边界值系统存在水平一致局部分支；建立了与时间水平无关的常数估计；验证了辛和Riccati准则可作为逆假设的充分条件；所有可镇定线性二次系统（包括非交换耦合数据）均被覆盖。

Conclusion: 该框架为有限时域庞特里亚金边界值系统提供了完备的水平一致局部分支理论，通过辛/Riccati准则简化了逆假设验证，数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: We study horizon-uniform local branches of finite-horizon discrete-time Pontryagin boundary value systems after smooth control elimination. The central input is a two-point endpoint inverse for the linearization. We verify this inverse from scaled stable--unstable boundary transversality, prove the associated endpoint-corrected Green estimate, and combine it with weighted contractions to obtain existence, uniqueness, Lipschitz dependence, and first-order expansions with constants independent of the horizon. The framework covers smooth nonlinear endpoint maps, including the original Pontryagin rows that fix the initial state and couple the terminal costate to the terminal state. Symplectic and Riccati criteria verify the inverse hypothesis at the level of the matrix data; in particular, every stabilizable linear-quadratic system with invertible dynamics and definite weights is covered, including noncommuting coupled data. A numerical section illustrates the certificates and the horizon-uniform first-order expansion.

</details>


### [126] [Operator Learning for PDE Backstepping Control of Parabolic Equations on Time-Varying Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17766)
*Jinrun Yan,Kai Liu,Zhong-Jie Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习（神经算子）的边界控制框架，用于稳定定义在时变空间域上的抛物型偏微分方程，通过神经算子逼近背步法设计算子，避免了在线重复求解时变核PDE，实现了近三个数量级的加速和实时稳定控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE背步法为时变空间域系统提供了系统化的理论框架，但其实时实现受限于需要在演化域上反复求解时变核PDE，计算成本高昂，难以满足实时控制需求。

Method: 1) 将时变背步法设计形式化为一个将移动边界轨迹映射到对应背步核的算子；2) 将时变核PDE域映射到固定参考域，建立核关于移动边界轨迹的连续依赖性，为神经算子逼近提供理论依据；3) 利用DeepONet离线学习核算子，在线部署生成时变核；4) 基于近似核算子构造边界反馈控制器。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法避免了在线重复求解时变核PDE，实现了相比传统数值核求解器近三个数量级的加速，从而能够在时变空间域上实现实时稳定控制。

Conclusion: 闭环系统在任意给定的有限时间区间上具有指数衰减估计；所提出的神经算子框架有效解决了时变背步法的实时实现难题。

Abstract: This paper develops a learning-based boundary control framework for stabilizing a parabolic equation defined on time-varying spatial domain. Although the partial differential equation (PDE) backstepping method provides a systematic theoretical framework for such moving-boundary systems, its real-time implementation is hindered by the need to repeatedly solve time-varying kernel PDEs on evolving domains. To overcome this limitation, we first formulate the time-varying backstepping design as an operator that maps the moving-boundary trajectory to the corresponding backstepping kernel. By mapping the time-varying domain of the backstepping kernel equation onto a fixed reference domain, we establish the continuous dependence of the kernel on the moving-boundary trajectory, which provides the theoretical basis for approximating the backstepping design operator by a neural operator. Based on the approximate kernel operator, we construct the corresponding boundary feedback controller to stabilize the system. It is shown that the closed-loop system admits an exponential decay estimate on any prescribed finite time interval. For numerical implementation, DeepONet is employed to learn the time-varying kernel operator from offline-generated numerical kernel solutions and is subsequently deployed online to generate the required time-varying kernels without repeatedly solving the kernel PDE. Numerical benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed neural-operator-based implementation bypasses repeated online solution of the time-varying kernel PDE, achieves a significant acceleration of close to three orders of magnitude compared with conventional numerical kernel solvers, and thus enables real-time stabilization of the system on time-varying spatial domain.

</details>


### [127] [Description of the set of admissible piecewise linear routes with n turns in the three dimensional case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17768)
*Nefedov V. N*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了连接空间中两点、由n+1段线段组成且每段转角有界的分段线性多边形链，给出了所有顶点所在区域的显式描述，并证明了该描述的可实现性，为枚举可行链和求解含路径代价与转弯代价的优化问题提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人路径规划、管道布线、车辆轨迹优化等应用场景中，路径通常由若干直线段组成，且转弯角度受机械或物理约束。现有研究对无转角约束的路径描述较为充分，但对转角有界且分段数固定的多边形链，其可行顶点集合的显式刻画尚不完整。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为后续算法设计提供数学基础。

Method: 本文采用几何分析方法，在条件 nφ ≤ π 下（即总累积转角不超过π），利用球面几何或空间向量约束推导多边形链所有内部顶点所在区域的边界条件。首先证明顶点集合的特征（定理1），再通过构造性引理（引理1）说明该集合中任意点均可作为实际路径顶点实现。进而将结果推广至所有顶点序列的联合可行域描述。

Result: 定理1给出了满足转角约束的多边形链所有内部顶点所属集合的精确描述；引理1证明了该集合中任意点都存在对应的可行多边形链；基于此，进一步得到了所有内部顶点序列 (B^{(1)}, ..., B^{(n)}) 的可行域显式公式。

Conclusion: 本文获得的多边形链顶点可行域显式描述，可用于设计枚举所有可行路径的算法，也可用于求解以路径长度和转弯代价为目标函数的优化问题。该结果为带转角约束的空间路径规划提供了严格的数学工具。

Abstract: We consider piecewise linear polygonal chains connecting two given points $A, B \in \mathbb{R}^3$ and consisting of exactly $n+1$ segments (i.e., having $n$ turning points). The absolute value of the turning angle at each interior point is bounded by a given number $\varphi \in (0,π)$. Under the condition $n\varphi \leq π$, we describe the set to which all interior vertices of such a polygonal chain belong (Theorem 1). It is proved that for any point $B^{(1)}$ from this set, there exists a polygonal chain with the specified parameters (Lemma 1). Based on these results, we obtain an explicit formula describing the set of all admissible sequences $(B^{(1)}, \ldots, B^{(n)})$ of the angular points of the polygonal chain. The obtained description can serve as a basis for constructing algorithms to enumerate admissible polygonal chains and to solve optimization problems for an objective function that accounts for the cost of traversing the segments and the cost of turns.

</details>


### [128] [A Bilevel Optimization Model for Bottom-Up Coordination of Multiple Low-Voltage Energy Communities and the Medium-Voltage Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17769)
*Fernando García-Muñoz,Sebastián Dávila,Luis Rojo-González,Cristian Duran-Mateluna*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Stackelberg博弈的上下级配电网分层协调框架，其中LV社区聚合商作为领导者、MV网络作为跟随者，通过LDD-ADMM分布式算法实现协调优化，在保护数据隐私的同时达到接近最优解的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式能源在低压配电网中的渗透率不断提高，LV社区（如能源社区）的发电、储能和能量交换活动需要与上游中压网络进行更有效的协调，而现有方法在处理离散决策和分布式隐私保护方面存在不足。

Method: 构建Stackelberg博弈结构：LV级市场运营者聚合多个社区作为领导者，确定DER调度和边界功率交换；MV网络作为跟随者，进行含常规机组和光伏的经济调度。LV级保留离散调度，MV级保持凸连续形式，通过KKT条件实现精确单层重构。同时提出LDD-ADMM分布式协调算法实现并行协调并保护数据隐私。

Result: 在IEEE 33节点MV系统和6个欧洲206节点LV馈线上验证，LDD-ADMM算法与精确KKT重构的平均相对偏差为1.7e-4，偏差仅出现在廉价资源稀缺时段。领导者决策可能导致跟随者成本高于其独立最优调度。

Conclusion: 所提分层协调框架能有效实现LV-MV协同优化，分布式算法在保持精度的同时保护隐私；但领导者可能将自身利益最大化而牺牲MV网络的经济性，需关注分层决策中的激励相容问题。

Abstract: The increasing penetration of distributed energy resources (DERs) is transforming low-voltage (LV) networks into active systems, including energy communities, whose generation, storage, and energy exchange activities require enhanced coordination with upstream medium-voltage (MV) networks. In the proposed Stackelberg structure, a local market operator aggregates multiple LV communities and acts as a single leader, determining DER operations and boundary energy exchanges, while the MV network serves as the follower, ensuring efficient system feasibility through an economic dispatch that includes both conventional and utility-scale PV generation. The proposed bottom-up coordination scheme concentrates discrete DER scheduling at the LV level while the MV level retains a convex continuous formulation, enabling an exact single-level reformulation via the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions. In addition, a distributed coordination algorithm that combines Lagrangian Dual Decomposition (LDD) with the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) is developed to coordinate LV communities in parallel while preserving data confidentiality. The framework is validated using the IEEE 33-bus system at the MV level and six European 206-bus LV test feeders. Results indicate that the LDD-ADMM algorithm closely matches the exact reformulation, with an average relative deviation of 1.7e-4, with deviations confined to periods of scarcity for the cheap resource. Furthermore, leaders' decisions can induce operating conditions that increase followers' costs relative to their independently optimal dispatch, a pattern reinforced by comparison with a feasibility-based single-level relaxation that satisfies the required energy exchanges but fails to achieve a cost-efficient allocation of MV resources.

</details>


### [129] [Optimizing the Principal Coefficient of Elliptic Equations using $L^p$-regularity, $p < \infty$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17788)
*Ala' Alalabi,Lorena Bociu,Paul Manns*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带总变分正则化和控制约束的椭圆型偏微分方程系数识别问题。通过利用W^{1,s}正则性建立控制-状态算子在L^q范数下的可微性，推导了约化目标函数的一阶和二阶可微性及最优性条件，并分析了基于L^r信任域的非光滑信任域方法，证明其收敛到一阶稳定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献依赖L^∞范数下的控制-状态算子连续性，这虽足以推导最优性条件，但不适用于数值算法，因为它忽略了扰动的空间范围，且与有限q的L^q范数存在定性差异。本文旨在弥补这一差距。

Method: 利用W^{1,s}正则性结果建立控制-状态算子在有限q的L^q范数下的可微性；基于此框架推导约化目标泛函的一阶和二阶可微性；通过限制次微分刻画总变差半范数并给出乘子正则性；分析基于L^r信任域（r>0.5q）的非光滑信任域方法。

Result: 建立了控制-状态算子在L^q范数下的可微性；推导了带总变分正则化的最优性条件；提出的非光滑信任域方法收敛到一阶稳定点。

Conclusion: 通过L^q框架克服了传统L^∞方法的局限性，为带总变分正则化的PDE约束优化问题提供了更适用于数值算法的理论基础，且信任域方法具有收敛性保证。

Abstract: We study coefficient identification problems for elliptic partial differential equations with total variation regularization and control constraints. Existing related literature relies on continuity and differentiability properties of the control-to-state operator with respect to the $L^\infty$-norm. While this is sufficient for deriving optimality conditions, it is not well-suited for numerical algorithms, as it neglects the spatial extent of perturbations and leads to a qualitative discrepancy compared to $L^q$-norms with $q < \infty$. In this work, we address this gap by exploiting $W^{1,s}$-regularity results to establish differentiability properties of the control-to-state operator with respect to $L^q$-norms for finite $q$. Based on this framework, we derive first- and second-order differentiability results for the reduced objective functional and establish first-order optimality conditions involving a restricted subdifferential characterization of the total variation seminorm and corresponding regularity of the associated multipliers. Building on this, we analyze a nonsmooth trust-region method based on an $L^r$-trust region for $r > 0.5 q$ and prove its convergence to first-order stationary points.

</details>


### [130] [Koopman Lifting with Certified Error Bounds for Joint Inference in Nonlinear Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17797)
*Chuansen Peng,Xiaojing Shen,Yunmin Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Koopman-GKFA框架，通过Koopman算子嵌入将非线性网络动力学提升为近似线性系统，结合组稀疏正则化的ADMM优化和卡尔曼滤波，实现非线性图动态系统中潜在节点状态与未知网络拓扑的联合推断，并在理论上证明了三个关键定理，实验上优于EKF、UKF和粒子滤波方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性图动态系统中，连续潜在节点状态与离散网络拓扑结构相互纠缠，准确恢复任意一方都严重依赖于另一方，现有方法缺乏统一的联合推断框架。

Method: 提出Koopman-GKFA框架：利用可分离节点字典的Koopman算子嵌入将非线性动力学提升为近似线性系统；采用组稀疏ADMM进行拓扑推断并具有线性收敛保证；引入指数遗忘因子追踪时变拓扑；通过卡尔曼滤波进行最优状态估计。

Result: 在Kuramoto振荡器、Hill动力学基因调控网络等合成基准以及NGSIM US-101、DREAM4真实数据集上，Koopman-GKFA在状态估计和拓扑恢复两方面均一致优于EKF、UKF和粒子滤波方法，且具有多项式计算复杂度和高维非线性鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上证明了结构同态引理（可分离字典条件下提升耦合算子的块稀疏性与图拓扑同构）、组稀疏ADMM的线性收敛性以及三项误差分解界（Koopman截断、观测噪声、拓扑残差），为非线性图动态系统的联合推断奠定了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: Jointly inferring latent node states and unknown network topology in nonlinear graphical dynamical systems is a fundamental yet largely unsolved problem, where the mutual entanglement of continuous states and discrete structure renders accurate recovery of either quantity critically dependent on the other. We propose \textbf{Koopman-GKFA} (Koopman Group-sparse Kalman Filter--ADMM), a unified framework that lifts nonlinear network dynamics into an approximately linear system via Koopman operator embedding with a separable node-wise dictionary, enabling optimal linear filtering for state estimation and provably convergent convex optimization for topology inference. Three theoretical contributions underpin the framework: (i)~a \emph{structural homomorphism lemma} proving that, under a separable-dictionary condition, block sparsity of the lifted coupling operator is isomorphic to the graph topology, providing the rigorous foundation for group-sparse regularization; (ii)~a block-structured group-sparse ADMM topology subproblem with certified linear convergence, extended by an exponential forgetting factor to track time-varying topologies; and (iii)~a \emph{three-term certified mean-squared error bound} that decomposes total estimation error into Koopman truncation, observation noise, and topology residual components, with monotone consistency established as the dictionary dimension grows. Extensive experiments on synthetic benchmarks (Kuramoto oscillators, Hill-kinetics gene-regulatory networks) and real-world datasets (NGSIM US-101, DREAM4) demonstrate that Koopman-GKFA consistently outperforms EKF-, UKF-, and particle-filter-based joint estimators in both state estimation and topology recovery, while exhibiting polynomial computational scaling and strong robustness in high-dimensional nonlinear settings.

</details>


### [131] [Kalman structure and observability for transport systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17814)
*Yacouba Simpore*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究常系数一阶双曲系统在线上的可观性与可控性，发现即使Kalman秩条件成立，标准L²估计也可能失效；Kalman结构决定合适的可观测性估计，部分分量需经代数步骤后可见，并在低傅里叶频率处以|ξ|^{2k}加权测量，同时推广了局部可观测性和Jordan块系统的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Kalman秩条件在偏微分方程框架下不足以保证标准L²可观测性估计成立，因为部分状态分量仅通过动力学间接检测，需要更精细的分析框架来刻画这类系统的可观测/可控性。

Method: 利用Kalman代数结构分析状态分量的可检测性层次，引入傅里叶频域加权（权重|ξ|^{2k}），构建Kalman适应观测空间；对可对角化系统证明局部可观测性，并扩展至具有实谱和Jordan块的系统。

Result: 证明了Kalman结构决定了合适的可观测性估计形式；分量经k步代数操作后可见，在低傅里叶频率处以|ξ|^{2k}加权；建立了Kalman适应的观测空间；对可对角化系统在观测集具有一致有界间隙时证明了局部可观测性；将全直线构造推广至实谱和Jordan块系统。

Conclusion: Kalman秩条件不足以直接保证标准L²可观测性，需根据Kalman结构引入加权观测空间；所得框架统一处理了对角化和非对角化（Jordan块）系统，并建立了局部可观测性结果。

Abstract: We study observability and controllability for constant-coefficient first-order hyperbolic systems on the real line when only part of the state is observed or controlled. Even when the Kalman rank condition holds, the usual \(L^2(\mathbb R)^N\)-observability estimate may fail because some components are detected only through the dynamics. We show that the Kalman structure determines the appropriate observability estimate. A component that becomes visible after \(k\) algebraic steps is measured at low Fourier frequencies with a weight of order \(|ξ|^{2k}\) in the Fourier variable. This yields a natural Kalman-adapted observation space for the system. We also prove localized observability for diagonalizable systems on observation sets with uniformly bounded gaps and, separately, extend the whole-line construction to systems with real spectrum and Jordan blocks.

</details>


### [132] [PepsiCo Deploys AI-Driven Pricing and Promotion Optimization at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17941)
*Aleix Llenas,Eduardo Salazar-Treviño,Francisco Leskovar,Francesc Pons-Llopis,Federico Todeschini,Deepika Gowda,David Bofill,Laxmi Anish,Michael Cleavinger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了百事公司部署的两套大规模优化系统——PromoAI和PricingAI，分别通过混合整数线性规划与机器学习结合、贝叶斯层级模型与非线性规划结合，优化促销日历和基础定价，实现数据驱动的收入增长管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度竞争和动态的市场中，企业需要同时考虑需求弹性、竞争对手行为、渠道和市场特定约束以及财务目标，传统手动方法无法应对高维度和相互依赖的复杂问题，因此需要基于运筹学的可扩展数据驱动决策支持系统。

Method: PromoAI：将基于机器学习的促销预测与混合整数线性规划模型结合，在数百万产品-促销-时机组合中搜索最大化百事和零售商收入的最优促销日历。PricingAI：使用贝叶斯层级模型估计自身和交叉价格弹性及竞争互动，再将其输入非线性规划引擎，推荐符合收入和利润目标约束的价格调整方案。

Result: 两套系统证明了先进优化技术在大规模企业环境中的可行性和可扩展性，实现了跨大型产品和客户组合的复杂规划流程优化。

Conclusion: 将统计学习与数学规划相结合，能够实现企业级自动化决策，既基于数据又符合战略业务目标，体现了运筹学在收入增长管理中的应用价值。

Abstract: Effective pricing and promotion planning constitutes a central pillar of strategic revenue management for firms operating in highly competitive and dynamic markets. These planning activities require the simultaneous consideration of demand elasticity, competitor actions, channel and market specific constraints, and financial objectives. As the dimensionality and interdependencies inherent in these problems increase, manual or traditional approaches become suboptimal and insufficient. In this context, Operations Research provides a robust methodological foundation for scalable, data-driven decision support systems that can optimize complex planning processes across large product and customer portfolios.
  This paper presents two large-scale optimization systems developed and deployed at PepsiCo to support Revenue Growth Management initiatives: PromoAI and PricingAI. PromoAI couples machine learning-based promotional forecasts with a mixed-integer linear programming model to optimize promotional calendars across trade channels, searching millions of product-promotion-timing combinations for the one that maximizes PepsiCo and retailer revenues subject to customizable business constraints. PricingAI optimizes base prices across product portfolios over multi-period horizons, using Bayesian hierarchical models to estimate own- and cross-price elasticities and competitive interactions, then feeding these into a nonlinear programming engine that recommends price changes aligned with revenue and margin targets under operational constraints.
  Together, these systems demonstrate the feasibility and scalability of advanced optimization in large-scale enterprise environments. They highlight the value of integrating statistical learning with mathematical programming to enable enterprise-level, automated decision-making that is both data-informed and aligned with strategic business objectives.

</details>

### [133] [Correct When Paired, Wrong When Split: Decoupling and Editing Modality-Specific Neurons in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17057)
*Tingchao Fu,Wenkai Wang,Fanxiao Li,Huadong Zhang,Jinhong Zhang,Dayang Li,Yunyun Dong,Renyang Liu,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大语言模型的知识编辑存在"编辑解耦失败"问题——当多模态输入（图文对）触发模型时知识可更新，但拆分为单模态输入时知识会回退到旧版本。原因是实体知识在MLLMs中并非统一存储，而是分散在分离的模态特定通路中。为此提出DECODE方法，显式解耦并定位模态特定的神经元群，实现不同模态触发下的一致知识更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法在多模态大语言模型中存在编辑解耦失败问题：多模态输入下知识可更新，但单模态输入下仍输出旧知识，表明知识未被统一存储。

Method: 提出DECODE，显式解耦并定位模态特定的神经元群，对不同模态通路中的目标知识进行针对性编辑。

Result: DECODE在不同模态触发条件下均能实现一致的知识更新，有效缓解编辑解耦失败问题。

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型的实体知识以模态解耦方式存储，DECODE通过定位模态特定神经元群实现跨模态一致的知识编辑。

Abstract: Although Knowledge Editing provides an efficient mechanism for updating the knowledge of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), we find that current paradigms still suffer from an important yet remain underexplored issue : editing decoupling failure, where entity-related knowledge can be updated when the model is triggered by multimodal inputs (text--image query pairs), however, it often reverts to outdated pre-edit facts when the paired inputs are split into unimodal ones. Our in-depth empirical analysis reveals that the entity knowledge in MLLMs is not stored as a unified representation, but is instead distributed across disentangled modality-specific pathways. As a result, updates biased toward multimodal queries fail to propagate effectively to unimodal circuits. To bridge this gap, we propose DECODE, which explicitly disentangles and localizes modality-specific neuron groups for targeted knowledge. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DECODE consistently achieves effective knowledge updates under different modality triggers, thereby mitigating editing decoupling failures.

</details>


### [134] [Diagnosing and Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17093)
*Adam Haroon,Anush Lakshman,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对远距离（>1m）单次条纹投影轮廓术中的信噪比下降和病态问题，本文通过机械可解释性（MI）和共形不确定性量化（UQ）诊断出基线UNet依赖物体边界形状先验而非条纹相位解码，并提出PhiCalNet——通过输出包裹相位并应用固定可微校准层将形状先验从假设空间中架构性移除，使物体MAE降低3.3倍（14.54→4.46 mm），MI与UQ一致确认了同一故障点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离单次FPP面临三个核心挑战：强度平方反比衰减导致条纹信噪比降低、物理真值退化；单张图像缺乏条纹阶次信息导致问题病态；现有架构缺乏机制层面的理解。

Method: 采用诊断-修复-验证框架：①用线性探针、Grad-CAM和平面外分布测试三个探针定位基线UNet依赖边界形状先验；②提出PhiCalNet，输出包裹相位+固定可微校准层映射为深度，从架构上消除形状先验解；③用共形UQ验证修复效果。

Result: PhiCalNet将物体MAE从14.54 mm降至4.46 mm（3.3倍提升）；残差主要由±π包裹不连续性处的0.103%像素引起；剔除前5%不确定像素后RMSE降低64%（20.6→7.4 mm），而基线仅降低3.5%。

Conclusion: MI和UQ作为收敛诊断工具一致识别出物理故障点；架构性修复（而非损失惩罚）是解决形状先验问题的关键；PhiCalNet显著提升了远距离FPP精度。

Abstract: Learning-based single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) has been studied mostly at close range. The long-range regime (standoff beyond 1 m) remains largely unaddressed: inverse-square intensity falloff lowers fringe signal-to-noise ratio and degrades physical ground truth, the single-shot problem is ill-posed because fringe-order information is absent from one image, and these architectures have not been studied mechanistically. We present a diagnose-repair-verify study using mechanistic interpretability (MI) and conformal uncertainty quantification (UQ) as convergent diagnostics: they agree on one physical failure locus, driving and verifying an architectural repair. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m), a best UNet baseline reaches 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE). Three probes (linear probing, Grad-CAM, flat-plane out-of-distribution test) converge: the baseline solves the task via object-boundary shape priors rather than fringe-phase decoding. We repair this with PhiCalNet, which outputs wrapped phase rather than depth and applies a fixed differentiable calibration layer mapping phase to depth, removing the shape-prior solution from the hypothesis space architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. A physics-informed loss that enforces the same physics as a soft penalty on a depth-regressing network yields no measurable gain, isolating the architecture as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm; the residual is carried by 0.103% of pixels at the +/-pi wrap discontinuity. Pixel-wise conformal UQ confirms the diagnosis: rejecting the top 5% of object pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts PhiCalNet RMSE by 64% (20.6->7.4 mm) versus 3.5% for the baseline. MI and UQ converge on the same failure locus.

</details>


### [135] [Informative Missingness to Generate Irregular Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17106)
*Hadi Mehdizavareh,Gabriele Santangelo,Giovanna Nicora,Simon Lebech Cichosz,Arianna Dagliati,Arijit Khan,Riccardo Bellazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的方法，用于联合建模临床时间序列中的实验室检测值和缺失模式。该方法扩展了TimeDiff框架，通过互补的扩散目标学习连续实验室值和离散缺失指示变量，在MIMIC-III的DACMI基准上验证了生成数据与真实患者轨迹的高度匹配，为后续训练能够利用信息性缺失的先验数据拟合网络奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录中的实验室检测数据采集不规律，检测缺失本身反映了临床决策和患者生理状态，具有信息性（类似MNAR缺失机制）。现有方法通常将缺失视为预处理伪影，忽视其临床意义，因此需要一种能够直接建模实验室值与缺失模式之间依赖关系的方法。

Method: 基于扩散模型扩展TimeDiff框架，将图表时间对齐为4小时间隔，将住院记录分割为7天窗口；为每个实验室值配对相应的观测指示变量；通过互补的扩散目标联合学习连续实验室值和离散缺失模式；应用标准化和归一化稳定训练。

Result: 生成数据在个体实验室分布和联合值-缺失嵌入空间上与真实患者轨迹高度匹配，证明扩散模型能够捕获患者生理状态与临床检测行为之间的临床有意义依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该模型可作为开发临床基础模型的初始组件，通过保留关键生理-缺失关系的合成先验，激励后续训练能够利用信息性缺失的先验数据拟合网络。

Abstract: Laboratory tests in electronic health records are collected irregularly, and the absence of a test order can be as informative as the measurement itself. Such missingness reflects clinicians' decisions and patient physiology, making it important to model it directly rather than treat it as a preprocessing artifact. Here we present a diffusion-based approach for generating clinical time series that jointly models laboratory values and their observation patterns using the public Data Analytics Challenge on Missing Data Imputation (DACMI) benchmark derived from MIMIC-III. To preserve realistic sampling, we align chart times into 4-hour intervals and segment admissions into 7-day windows, producing trajectories that pair each lab value with a corresponding observation indicator. Standard transformations and normalization are applied to stabilize training. Our method extends the TimeDiff framework to learn continuous lab values and discrete missingness patterns through complementary diffusion objectives. Experiments show that the generated data closely match real patient trajectories across individual lab distributions and joint value-missingness embeddings, demonstrating that diffusion models can capture clinically meaningful dependencies between patient physiology and clinicians' testing behavior under MNAR-like (missing-not-at-random) missingness. These preliminary results indicate that our model can serve as an initial component toward developing clinical foundation models. By producing synthetic priors that preserve key physiology-missingness relationships, this work motivates the subsequent training of Prior-Data Fitted Networks capable of leveraging informative missingness, which we will investigate in the extended work.

</details>


### [136] [Models Take Notes at Prefill: KV Cache Can Be Editable and Composable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17107)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer的prefix KV缓存中，前缀共享的缓存仅通过改写被修改字段自身的key/value即可复用，因为模型在prefill阶段已将字段条件结论写入下游"笔记"（attention层输出），而字段自身的key/value对决策贡献不足1%。基于此提出两个能力：(1) 可编辑性：通过追加"勘误"修改笔记，结合CoT可在仅用~1%计算量下恢复决策；(2) 可组合性：笔记位置可移植，预编译技能可通过RoPE重定位拼接到任意上下文，实现O(L)而非O(L^2)的首令牌延迟。统一编辑+组合代理在保持决策一致的同时实现最高14.9倍延迟降低，并在vLLM在线基准测试中保持98.5%前缀缓存命中率，p90 TTFT降低53-398倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有prefix caching仅对完全共享前缀复用prefill结果，一个字段变更会导致整个下游缓存失效。作者希望探究能否在仅修改变更字段自身KV的情况下复用其余缓存，并揭示其因果关系。

Method: 通过因果分析跨4个模型家族建立机制：prefill阶段模型已将字段条件结论写入下游attention层（"笔记"），字段自身KV对最终决策贡献<1%。基于此提出两种操作：(1) 编辑：追加"勘误"修改笔记，结合CoT恢复决策；(2) 组合：利用笔记的位置可移植性，通过RoPE重定位将预编译技能拼接到任意上下文。

Result: 编辑操作在8B模型上用~1%计算量恢复决策（1.00准确率）；组合操作在12个模型上达到logit cosine相似度0.90-0.999，时间复杂度O(L)而非O(L^2)；统一代理实现最高14.9倍延迟降低；vLLM在线基准测试中保持98.5%前缀缓存命中率，p90 TTFT降低53-398倍。

Conclusion: Transformer的prefix KV缓存可被视为"笔记"（memoized conclusions）的集合，具备可编辑性和可组合性，适用于任意per-token attention KV缓存，跨尺度、量化、MoE、多模态缓存均有效，可通过小适配器扩展到多种注意力变体。勘误采用追加方式，与生产级prefix caching兼容。

Abstract: Prefix caching reuses prefill only across an exactly shared prefix, so one changed field invalidates the entire downstream cache. Yet overwriting the field's own key/value vectors and reusing the rest leaves the model acting on the old value. The reason, established causally across four model families: at prefill the model has already written the field-conditioned conclusion onto downstream notes; the field's own key/value drives under 1% of the decision. Read as a notebook of memoized conclusions, two capabilities follow. (1) It is editable. A salient erratum amends the notes; and with chain-of-thought, editing the field alone recovers the decision (1.00 at 8B, ~1% compute), while without CoT it is ignored. (2) It is composable. The notes are position-portable, so a precompiled skill can be RoPE-repositioned and spliced into any context, indistinguishable from full recompute (logit cosine 0.90-0.999, twelve models) at O(L) rather than O(L^2) time-to-first-token. A unified edit+compose agent stays decision-identical to recompute at up to 14.9x lower latency. The approach applies to any per-token attention KV cache, validated across scale, quantization, Mixture-of-Experts, and multimodal caches, and extends to several attention variants through small adapters. Because the erratum is append-only, it composes with production prefix caching: in an online vLLM benchmark it keeps the prefix cache-aligned (98.5% hit-rate), cutting p90 time-to-first-token by 53-398x.

</details>


### [137] [The Critical Role of Model Selection in Causal Inference: A Comparative Analysis of Classification Models within the InferBERT Framework for Pharmacovigilance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17113)
*Csaba Kiss,Roland Molontay,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了InferBERT框架中不同模型选择（XGBoost、ALBERT、BioBERT、Med-LLaMA）对因果药物不良事件检测的影响，发现在两个基准数据集上，领域特定预训练的BioBERT在准确率、校准误差和因果一致性方面均优于简单基线和大规模LLM（Med-LLaMA），表明在计算药物警戒中投资领域感知的中等规模模型比单纯扩大模型规模更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果药物不良事件（ADE）与虚假相关性之间的区分是药物警戒的核心挑战。InferBERT框架虽整合了Transformer与Do-calculus，但其成功依赖于底层分类模型的选择。目前缺乏对不同模型选择（从简单基线到大规模LLM）在因果ADE检测中表现的系统性比较研究。

Method: 在Analgesics-induced Acute Liver Failure (AILF)和Tramadol-related Mortalities (TRAM)两个基准数据集上，对四种模型（XGBoost基线、ALBERT原始InferBERT、BioBERT生物医学Transformer、Med-LLaMA医学大语言模型）进行5折交叉验证重复20次，测量准确率、校准误差（ECE，含等渗回归前后）以及因果项与PRR/ROR/EBGM的Jaccard一致性，并通过配对t检验进行显著性检验。

Result: BioBERT在两个数据集上均取得最高准确率；Med-LLaMA尽管模型规模大且采用参数高效微调，表现却最差；领域特定预训练是关键决定因素；校准改善了ECE但对准确率和因果发现影响不一；BioBERT与传统药物警戒信号的一致性最强。

Conclusion: 领域特定预训练相较于简单基线和更大规模LLM具有明显优势。在计算药物警戒中，投资于可管理的、领域感知的模型比单纯扩大模型规模更为有效。

Abstract: Distinguishing causal adverse drug events (ADEs) from spurious correlations remains a central challenge in pharmacovigilance. The InferBERT framework integrates transformer models with Do-calculus, but its success hinges on the underlying classification model. This study evaluates the impact of model choice in InferBERT, assessing whether simpler models suffice, if domain-specific pre-training helps, whether scaling to LLMs improves causal detection, and the effect of post-hoc calibration. We performed a comparative study on two benchmarks: Analgesics-induced Acute Liver Failure (AILF) and Tramadol-related Mortalities (TRAM). Four models were evaluated-XGBoost (baseline), ALBERT (original InferBERT), BioBERT (biomedical transformer), and Med-LLaMA (medical LLM)-using 5-fold cross-validation repeated over 20 runs. We measured accuracy, Expected Calibration Error (ECE) pre- and post-isotonic regression, and Jaccard concordance of causal terms with PRR, ROR, and EBGM; significance was tested with paired t-tests. BioBERT achieved the highest accuracy on both datasets, while Med-LLaMA underperformed despite its size and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Domain-specific pre-training was decisive. Calibration improved ECE but had mixed effects on accuracy and causal discovery. BioBERT's superiority also yielded the strongest concordance with traditional pharmacovigilance signals. These results show that domain-specific pre-training provides a clear advantage over simpler baselines and larger LLMs. Investing in manageable, domain-aware models is more effective for computational pharmacovigilance than simply scaling model size.

</details>


### [138] [Probing, Fusion, and Trustworthiness: A Systematic Evaluation of Foundation Model Representations for Multimodal Cancer Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17115)
*Jingyu Hu,Giuseppe Tripodi,Reed Naidoo,Sarah F. McGough,Tapabrata Chakraborti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基础模型在病理学任务中的泛化能力，发现图像与组学表示具有互补性，多模态融合在单模态信号不占优时有效，且符合预测能提升临床决策的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在医学数据中已展现出强大的表示提取能力，但它们在分布偏移下的泛化性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在IH-BC和IH-NSCLC两个真实商业队列上，对五种基础模型进行单模态探测基准测试，并比较三种图像-组学融合策略，最后通过符合预测评估可信度。

Result: 基础模型表示在分布外数据上表现优异；多模态融合主要在单模态信号不占优时带来增益；符合预测显示大多数预测失败病例的真相仍可在预测集中恢复。

Conclusion: 基础模型表示在分布偏移下具有竞争力，多模态融合的收益取决于模态间的信号强弱关系，不确定性感知推理对临床支持具有重要价值。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have emerged as powerful representation extractors for medical data, yet their generalizability to datasets under distribution shift remains underexplored. This work systematically evaluates FM-based representations on a suite of computational pathology tasks across two real-world commercial cohorts, IH-BC and IH-NSCLC, drawn from the licensed in-house (IH) oncology dataset. The analysis focuses on two modalities, whole-slide images and transcriptomic profiles, drawn from the IH multimodal data. We first benchmark unimodal probing performance across five FMs on eight downstream classification tasks, and find that image and omics representations carry complementary predictive signals. Then we investigate whether multimodal fusion can yield additional gains over unimodal baselines by comparing three image-omics fusion strategies built on paired representations. The trustworthiness of selected unimodal and multimodal pipelines is further assessed through conformal prediction. Our results show that FM representations achieve competitive performance on out-of-distribution data and that multimodal fusion helps mainly when no single modality dominates the signal. Conformal prediction reveals that in the majority of cases where a point prediction fails, the true diagnosis remains recoverable within the prediction set, reinforcing the value of uncertainty-aware inference for clinical support.

</details>


### [139] [MODE: Modality-Decomposed Expert-Level Mixed-Precision Quantization for MoE Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17118)
*Yuanteng Chen,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Nanxin Zeng,Yuantian Shao,Shiqiang Lang,Tao Liu,Chuangyi Li,Qinghao Hu,Gang Li,Jing Liu,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MODE，一种面向MoE多模态大语言模型的模态分解专家级混合精度量化框架，通过分解专家选择频率、过滤冗余视觉token并整合模态级量化灵敏度，在W3A16设置下将平均性能损失控制在2.9%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE-MLLMs推理时GPU显存开销极大，亟需压缩。现有专家级混合精度量化方法在MoE-LLMs上有效，但在MoE-MLLMs上性能显著下降，原因是专家重要性估计中存在两个被忽视的偏差：(1)跨模态层面，视觉token的数量优势使得专家选择频率被视觉token主导，掩盖了对文本模态关键的专家；(2)视觉内部层面，大量冗余视觉token进一步扭曲频率统计，遮蔽了对信息性视觉内容重要的专家。

Method: (1)按模态分解专家选择频率；(2)过滤冗余视觉token以获取去噪后的视觉频率；(3)评估每个模态的量化灵敏度作为频率估计的补充信号；(4)将这些信号整合到整数线性规划(ILP)中，在给定预算下为每个专家分配比特宽度。

Result: MODE在W3A16设置下将平均性能损失限制在2.9%以内，在极端2比特设置下获得更大的性能增益。

Conclusion: MODE特别适合MoE-MLLMs的混合精度量化，有效解决了现有方法在跨模态和视觉内部层面的偏差问题。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE-MLLMs) offer remarkable performance but incur prohibitive GPU memory costs, making compression essential. Among PTQ methods, expert-level mixed-precision quantization has proven effective for MoE-LLMs, yet suffers notable degradation on MoE-MLLMs due to two overlooked biases in expert importance estimation. (1) At the cross-modal level, the numerical dominance of vision tokens causes expert selection frequency to be dominated by vision tokens, masking experts that are critical to the text modality; (2) at the intra-vision level, the large proportion of redundant vision tokens further skew frequency statistics, obscuring experts critical for informative visual content. To bridge gaps, we propose MODE, a modality-decomposed expert-level mixed-precision quantization framework for MoE-MLLMs that decomposes expert selection frequency by modality, filters redundant vision tokens to obtain denoised visual frequency, and further evaluates quantization sensitivity per modality as a complementary signal to frequency-based estimation. These signals are integrated into an Integer Linear Programming formulation to assign per-expert bit-widths under a given budget. Extensive experiments show that MODE is particularly well-suited for MoE-MLLMs, limiting average performance loss to within 2.9% at W3A16, with larger gains at the extreme 2-bit setting.

</details>


### [140] [Noise-Driven Escape from Metastable Phases explains Grokking in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17120)
*Ibrahim Talha Ersoy,Karoline Wiesner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示深度神经网络中的“顿悟（grokking）”现象源于L2正则化引起的一阶相变中的滞回效应：模型被困在低精度的亚稳态中，需依靠SGD噪声越过能垒才能逃逸，逃逸时间服从阿伦尼乌斯标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释深度学习中grokking现象（突然的、延迟的泛化能力涌现）的起源，并阐明其与L2正则化相变及滞回效应的内在联系。

Method: 在线性DNN中，通过L2正则化刻意将模型困在低精度亚稳态，利用SGD噪声驱动其越过能垒；采用稀疏子采样再现经典grokking曲线；分析数据协方差奇异值对应的亚稳态数量，并推广至非线性DNN。

Result: 1) grokking是L2一阶相变中滞回效应的表现；2) 逃逸时间在近两个数量级内服从阿伦尼乌斯标度律；3) 亚稳态数量等于数据协方差的可学习特征数（每个奇异值对应一个）；4) 在非线性DNN中也观察到类似机制。

Conclusion: 滞后效应随任务复杂度自然增长，该发现为设计更高效的学习方案提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) exhibit first order phase transitions under variations of the L2 regularization strength, with each transition marking the onset of a new learnable feature. Below a critical regularization strength, all features are in principle learnable, but coexisting metastable states, separated by energy barriers, can trap the network and impede convergence. A strength of DNNs is their ability to generalize. But many open questions remain, among them the origin of so called grokking: the abrupt, delayed onset of generalization after prolonged apparent overfitting. We show for linear DNNs that grokking is consistent with hysteresis in first-order L2 phase transitions: using L2 regularization to engineer deliberate trapping, we demonstrate that a model in a low-accuracy metastable state escapes only when SGD noise drives it across an energy barrier, with escape times following Arrhenius scaling. We reproduce grokking-like delayed convergence across two orders of magnitude in escape time by deliberately trapping models in metastable phases. Using sparse sub-sampling we also reproduce the canonical grokking curve where test error eventually approaches the final training error. Our work suggests that the number of metastable states equals the number of learnable features -- one per singular value of the data covariance -- the potential for hysteresis grows naturally with task complexity. We provide evidence that the same mechanism likely operates in general nonlinear DNNs. Our results provide routes toward more efficient learning schemes.

</details>


### [141] [Towards Fast GNN Surrogates for CO2 Migration in Complex Geological Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17180)
*Rodrigo S. Luna,Thiago H. N. Coelho,Luiz S. L. Neto,Roberto M. Velho,Adriano M. A. Cortes,Renato N. Elias,Alexandre G. Evsukoff,Fernando A. Rochinha,Mauricio Araya-Polo,Herve Gross,Alvaro L. G. A. Coutinho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端图神经网络替代模型，用于地质封存中CO₂羽流迁移预测，在SPE11A基准测试上实现了对气体饱和度和液相密度的竞争性预测，累积误差在长时间范围内保持适中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值模拟CO₂地质封存中的多相流行为计算成本高昂，亟需一种数据驱动的、能高效再现复杂物理行为的替代模型。

Method: 将SPE11A基准重构成图结构（节点=计算单元，边=基于传导率的交互），通过各向异性消息传递机制（几何条件边嵌入偏置消息聚合方向）捕获方向性输运，采用自回归残差公式在潜空间建模时间演化，并使用多步监督训练。

Result: 在气体饱和度和液相密度预测上取得竞争性结果，累计误差在扩展预测范围内保持适中水平。

Conclusion: 基于图神经网络的替代模型能够有效再现复杂地质储层中CO₂多相流的关键物理行为，为碳封存监测提供高效预测工具。

Abstract: This chapter discusses how a data-driven machine learning approach can reproduce key aspects of the physical behavior of multiphase flows in complex geological formations. We propose an end-to-end graph neural surrogate tailored to CO$_2$ plume migration forecasting in geological storage. The method is evaluated on the SPE11A benchmark, a well-known industry test case designed to assess CO$_2$ storage scenarios and characterized by sharp gas-water interfaces, strong advective transport, and rapid convective mixing with fingering development. The benchmark is reformulated as a graph in which nodes represent computational cells and edges encode transmissibility-based interactions enriched with geometric attributes. Directional transport arising from grid geometry, permeability contrasts, and geological heterogeneity is captured through an anisotropic message-passing mechanism, where interaction weights are computed via geometry-conditioned edge embeddings, biasing message aggregation toward physically relevant transport directions. Temporal evolution is modeled in latent space using an autoregressive residual formulation trained with multi-step supervision. The proposed model produces competitive forecasts of gas saturation and liquid-phase density, which are key indicators for CO$_2$ storage monitoring, with cumulative errors that remain moderate over extended forecasting horizons.

</details>


### [142] [Verified Detection and Prevention of Concurrency Anomalies in Multi-Agent Large Language Model Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17182)
*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化验证了多智能体LLM系统中的四种并发异常（stale-generation, phantom-tool, causal-cascade, tool-effect reordering），构建了首个机器校验的一致性层级L0⊊⋯⊊L4，通过274个Verus验证义务（零假设/零承认）证明了检测器的健全性和完备性，并在三个部署的Rust运行时中实现了L0-L2，同时复现了ByteDance deer-flow中的静默丢失更新和LangGraph ToolNode中的工具效应重排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过内存存储、向量索引和工具注册表共享状态，这种共享本质上是长时间运行的读-生成-写操作，在确定性执行引擎（通过确定性重放强制执行的持久执行引擎）下存在并发一致性问题，但缺乏形式化的分析和验证框架。

Method: 在TLA+中形式化了四种并发异常（stale-generation, phantom-tool, causal-cascade, tool-effect reordering），作为经典隔离异常的结构类比，构建了排除格（exclusion lattice）中的一个最大链L0⊊⋯⊊L4。通过274个Verus验证义务（零assume、零admit，信任基仅为两个结构公理和一个互斥对应关系）证明检测器对规约的健全性和完备性。三个部署的Rust运行时分别实现L0-L1（悲观锁、可序列化快照隔离、默认SI），L2-L4通过执行模式验证并采用无依赖预防孪生（A3, A6, A2: 0/1000 vs 1000/1000）。

Result: L0-L1在三个部署的Rust运行时中实现并通过验证；L2在三个模型家族上的120个撤回会话中全部预防了A3异常；复现了ByteDance deer-flow的静默丢失更新并将其修复形式化为L0→L1精化；展示了LangGraph ToolNode中未修改输出的工具效应重排序，通过L3提交排序器消除。

Conclusion: 验证的检测器、精化和可实现性工件是主要贡献；异常现象和格是经典的。这是首个针对此类运行时的机器校验一致性层级。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems share state through memory stores, vector indices, and tool registries. We model such sharing as long-running read-generate-write operations under deterministic-generation semantics -- the regime durable-execution engines enforce by deterministic replay -- and formalize four concurrency anomalies in TLA+: stale-generation, phantom-tool, causal-cascade, and tool-effect reordering, structural analogues of classical isolation anomalies, each with a TLC counter-example. The exclusion lattice over these anomalies is trivial; the contribution is the mechanically verified realizability and strict separation of one maximal chain within it, $L_0 \subsetneq \cdots \subsetneq L_4$, to our knowledge the first machine-checked consistency hierarchy for such runtimes. A development of 274 Verus obligations (zero assume, zero admit; trust base: two structural axioms and a mutex correspondence) proves the detectors sound and complete against the specifications and each runtime its avoidance set. Three deployed Rust runtimes realize L0-L1 (pessimistic locking, serializable snapshot isolation, default-SI), each verified against stale-generation and refined to its state machine; L2-L4 are exec-mode-verified with dependency-free prevention twins (A3, A6, A2: 0/1000 versus 1000/1000), and L2 is run live across three model families (A3 prevented in all 120 retracted sessions). We reproduce a silent lost update in ByteDance's deer-flow, formalizing its fix as a verified $L_0 \to L_1$ refinement, and exhibit tool-effect reordering in LangGraph's ToolNode on unmodified output, removed by an L3 commit-order sequencer. The verified detector, refinements, and realizability artifacts are the contribution; the phenomena and lattice are classical.

</details>


### [143] [Finsler Geometry, Graph Neural Networks, and You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17185)
*T. Mitchell Roddenberry,Richard G. Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于芬斯勒拉普拉斯算子的图神经网络架构，证明其对流形上点云采样的离散估计收敛性，并展示其在非线性扩散方程几何恢复中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图拉普拉斯的图神经网络只能逼近各向同性算子（拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米算子），缺乏处理各向异性（非线性）扩散的能力。本文旨在引入芬斯勒拉普拉斯算子作为非线性替代，扩展图神经网络的表达能力。

Method: 从流形上点云采样估计芬斯勒拉普拉斯算子，证明离散估计收敛到真实算子；将该算子表达为图神经网络层，构建受芬斯勒几何约束的图神经网络家族。

Result: 证明了离散芬斯勒拉普拉斯估计在点云数量增长时收敛到流形上的真实算子；芬斯勒图神经网络能够恢复非线性扩散方程背后的几何结构。

Conclusion: 芬斯勒图神经网络作为各向异性图神经网络，突破了传统图拉普拉斯方法的各向同性限制，能有效处理非线性扩散问题，为图神经网络在几何深度学习中的应用提供了新方向。

Abstract: Graph neural network architectures based on the graph Laplacian approximate the Laplace-Beltrami operator, thus limiting their application to isotropic operators. As a nonlinear alternative to the Laplace-Beltrami operator, we consider estimates of the Finsler Laplacian on point clouds sampled from a manifold. We prove that these discrete estimates converge to the true operator on the manifold as the number of point samples grows. Moreover, we show that this operator can be expressed as a graph neural network layer, which we use to define a family of Finslerian graph neural networks constrained to express Finsler geometry. We show that Finslerian graph neural networks recover the geometry underlying nonlinear diffusion equations in practice.

</details>


### [144] [Constrained Diffusion Models with Primal-Dual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17192)
*Samar Hadou,Yigit Berkay Uslu,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于原始-对偶推理（PDI）的约束扩散模型，用于从具有平均约束的熵正则化优化问题的最优分布中采样。PDI在扩散反向过程中联合推断最优原始分布及其对偶变量，通过对偶上升更新对偶变量，实验证明其在混合高斯、无线资源分配和投资组合管理任务上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束扩散模型通常先估计对偶乘子再冻结进行采样，无法灵活适应约束变化，且对偶变量与采样过程割裂。本文旨在实现约束扩散过程中原始分布与对偶变量的联合推断。

Method: 在拉格朗日对偶域中形式化约束采样问题，最优分布为以最优对偶变量为参数的吉布斯分布。训练一个以对偶变量为条件的得分网络，覆盖推理过程中遇到的所有吉布斯分布族；每个反向扩散步骤先用当前对偶变量对应的得分场去噪，再通过估计去噪样本的约束违反程度进行对偶上升更新对偶变量。

Result: 理论证明推理轨迹上对偶变量的时间平均值收敛到对偶最优值的一个邻域，并通过调度相关的稳定性因子界定了残差对偶不匹配对终端分布的影响。实验在混合高斯约束采样、无线资源分配和投资组合管理任务上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: PDI实现了约束扩散模型中原始采样与对偶优化的联合推理，为带平均约束的熵正则化优化问题提供了一种有效的采样方法，具有良好的理论保证和实际应用效果。

Abstract: This paper develops constrained diffusion models with primal-dual inference (PDI) to sample from optimal distributions of entropy-regularized optimization problems with \emph{average} constraints. We formalize constrained sampling in the Lagrangian dual domain, where the optimal distribution takes the form of a Gibbs distribution indexed by the optimal dual variable. Rather than estimating this dual multiplier before sampling and freezing it throughout generation, PDI jointly infers the optimal primal distribution and its parametrizing dual variable. Each reverse diffusion step denoises using the score field associated with the current multiplier and then updates the multiplier through dual ascent using the estimated constraint violation of the denoised samples. To enable this conditional score field, we train a single dual-conditioned score network over the family of Gibbs distributions induced by the dual variables encountered during inference. We prove that the time average of the dual variables generated along the inference trajectory converges to a neighborhood of the dual optimum and bound the effect of residual dual mismatch on the terminal distribution through schedule-dependent stability factors. We evaluate PDI on constrained sampling from a mixture of Gaussians, wireless resource allocation, and portfolio management.

</details>


### [145] [PowerOPD: Stabilizing On-Policy Distillation with Bounded Power Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17199)
*Anhao Zhao,Junlong Tong,Yingqi Fan,Ping Nie,Wenjie Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准在线蒸馏（OPD）中基于log-ratio的奖励函数存在无界、高方差问题，导致训练不稳定。本文提出PowerOPD，通过Box-Cox幂变换构造有界且符号一致的奖励函数，在数学推理任务上显著超越OPD，同时降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线蒸馏（OPD）使用的学生采样token的蒙特卡洛估计器存在严重训练病态：样本效率低、生成动态不稳定、与全词汇OPD存在显著性能差距。诊断发现根源在于log-ratio奖励函数无界，导致梯度方差极大且持续存在于整个训练过程中，后置缩放方法无法解决。

Method: 提出PowerOPD，基于Box-Cox幂变换构造一族原生有界、符号一致的奖励函数，由参数α>0控制。当α→0时退化为log-ratio。该方法替代标准OPD中的无界log-ratio奖励。

Result: 在6个数学推理基准和4个Qwen3师生对上的实验显示：PowerOPD相比vanilla OPD在Avg@8/Pass@8上分别提升+6.37/+5.71，相比后置稳定化提升+3.01/+3.54，相比全词汇OPD提升+2.59/+8.90；训练时间减少59.2%，GPU峰值内存减少23.1%；梯度范数比vanilla OPD小3000倍以上。

Conclusion: PowerOPD通过有界奖励函数从根本上解决了在线蒸馏中log-ratio奖励的高方差问题，在提升性能的同时显著降低了计算资源消耗。

Abstract: Standard on-policy distillation (OPD) for large language models estimates the reverse-KL objective using student-sampled tokens, yielding an unbiased single-sample Monte Carlo estimator that avoids vocabulary-wide computation. However, we show that this estimator suffers from severe training pathologies in practice: sample inefficiency, unstable generation dynamics, and a substantial performance gap compared to exact full-vocabulary OPD. Reward-level diagnosis traces these pathologies to the log-ratio reward, which is unbounded by construction, producing extremely high-variance gradients concentrated at early positions and persisting throughout training; standard post-hoc scaling fail as they operate only after this distortion occurs. To solve this problem, we propose PowerOPD: a family of natively bounded, sign-consistent rewards from the Box-Cox power transformation, parameterized by alpha > 0, of which the log-ratio is the degenerate alpha -> 0 limit. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks and four Qwen3 teacher-student pairs, PowerOPD achieves benchmark-averaged Avg@8/Pass@8 gains of up to +6.37/+5.71 over vanilla OPD, +3.01/+3.54 over post-hoc stabilization, and +2.59/+8.90 over full-vocabulary OPD, while reducing wall-clock time by 59.2% and peak GPU memory by 23.1%. Larger alpha generally improves accuracy, consistently shortens responses, and keeps gradient norms more than 3,000x smaller than vanilla OPD.

</details>


### [146] [Sum-of-Squares Degree Barriers for the Reweighted-Hinge Method in Robust Halfspace Learning: A Christoffel-Function Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17215)
*Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了离群点移除证书的盲区大小恰好由清洁分布的Christoffel函数决定，并以此为核心原理，系统分析了基于重加权铰链的鲁棒半空间学习方法的SoS度数与恶意噪声容忍度之间的精确权衡，推导出边际-度数折中、度数-2障碍以及度数-2t算法前沿三个结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒学习方法中，基于矩的离群点移除证书存在盲区——数据仅通过低阶矩被观测，攻击者可在清洁数据已有典型特征的区域隐藏污染，而该盲区的精确大小尚未被量化。本文欲从对抗视角出发，将Christoffel函数（通常用于离群检测）重新解释为有界度数证书无法移除的污染质量上限，从而建立鲁棒学习的统一组织原理。

Method: 采用重加权铰链（Reweighted Hinge）方法，以Sum-of-Squares度数作为离群点移除证书的核心资源，将Christoffel函数作为度量指标。通过对密度薄饼（dense pancake）的构造和加权Chebyshev约简，推导出度数-边际-误差之间的精确关系；通过显式实例构造度数-2障碍和度数-2t算法边界。

Result: 1) 边际-度数折中：证书密度薄饼至误差ε需要SoS度数为Ω(log(1/ε))或边距为Ω(√(log(1/ε))/√d)；2) 度数-2障碍：度数为2时故障率被卡在η^{1/2}，而度数4可突破该界限；3) 度数-2t算法：前沿为η^{1-1/2t}，恢复Shen(2025)在t=1时的结果，且常数由薄饼密度上限决定，度数-2障碍证明其不可改进。

Conclusion: Christoffel函数精确刻画了有界度数证书的盲区大小，为基于SoS的鲁棒学习提供了统一的理论框架。边际-度数折中解释了现有方法中边距随log(1/ε)增长的必要性，度数-2障碍揭示了方法的小故障率由度数而非分析技巧决定，度数-2t算法则描绘了可实现的鲁棒性前沿。

Abstract: A certificate that removes outliers sees the data only through its low-degree moments, and an adversary exploits exactly this, hiding corruption where the clean data already looks typical, in the blind spot no bounded-degree test resolves. That blind spot turns out to have an exact size: the Christoffel function of the clean marginal, the very quantity modern data analysis thresholds to detect outliers, here read from the adversary's side as the corruption a bounded-degree certificate cannot remove.
