We turn this inversion into the organizing principle of the reweighted-hinge approach to robustly learning $γ$-margin halfspaces under malicious noise (Shen, 2025; Zeng and Shen, 2025): the governing resource is the Sum-of-Squares degree of the outlier-removal certificate, and the resolution principle states that the maximal corruption mass which can hide at a center $c$ from a degree-$2t$ certificate is exactly the Christoffel function $λ_{t+1}(c)$ of the clean marginal. Three consequences follow, all against the certificate method (not information-theoretic). A margin-degree tradeoff: certifying the dense pancake to error $ε$ costs SoS degree $Ω(\log(1/ε))$ or margin $Ω(\sqrt{\log(1/ε)}/\sqrt{d})$, explaining why the $\log(1/ε)$ margin Shen (2025) records is forced, with a weighted-Chebyshev reduction making the threshold $2t=Θ((|c|/s)^2)$ tight modulo one classical weighted-extremal estimate. A degree-$2$ outlier barrier: the resolution principle realized as an explicit instance on which degree $2$ is stuck at $η^{1/2}$ while degree $4$ escapes, locating the method's small breakdown rate in the degree, not the analysis. And a degree-$2t$ algorithm tracing the frontier $η^{1-1/2t}$ (recovering Shen (2025) at $t=1$), whose gain is an explicit constant, capped by the pancake density and shown unimprovable by the degree-$2$ barrier.

</details>


### [147] [Rift: A Conflict Signature for Deception in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17229)
*Petr Nyoma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过对比“知道真相却说谎”的潜伏代理人与“同样输出错误答案但不知真相”的 naive 说谎者，发现说谎行为在内部表征中留下独特的“冲突签名”（residual rank 高出 2.1-2.3 倍），该签名可 100% 准确识别谎言，跨模型、跨架构、跨语言迁移，且只读不可注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ELK 问题中，行为评估无法区分“知道真相但说谎”与“诚实的错误”，本文旨在寻找一个能区分这两种情况的内部表征信号。

Method: 引入“naive liar”作为控制组（经微调输出相同错误答案但无诚实训练），对比潜伏代理人的前向传播；计算 residual rank 等内部表征指标，并验证其在不同模型、语言、架构及主动隐藏场景下的鲁棒性。

Result: 欺骗性前向传播的 residual rank 比 naive liar 高 2.1-2.3 倍；在多种模型中谎言检测 AUC 达 1.0；跨模型族零样本迁移 AUC 均值 0.933；跨五种语言 AUC 1.0；该签名只可检测不可注入。

Conclusion: 欺骗行为在模型内部留下可泛化的冲突签名，可用于可靠检测“知道真相却撒谎”的隐蔽欺骗，且该签名对模型行为不可控（只读性）。

Abstract: A model that lies while knowing the truth is the central case ELK cannot handle with behavioral evaluation alone. We ask whether such deception leaves an internal signature distinguishing it from honest error. Our key move is a control for wrongness: we contrast a sleeper agent (knows the truth, lies on trigger) against a naive liar (fine-tuned to emit the same wrong answers with no honest training). Both produce identical wrong outputs; any difference is about knowledge conflict, not incorrectness. We find deceptive forward passes carry a conflict signature - 2.1-2.3x higher residual rank than naive-liar passes on the same wrong answer - strong enough to identify which of two responses is the lie with 100% accuracy and no labels, across GPT-2 small/medium (three seeds) and three instruct models. Across Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B and Phi-3-mini, instructed deception raises residual rank on every tested fact (18/18, 40/40, 34/34); on Phi-3, lies separate perfectly from both honest answers and hallucinations (AUC 1.0, Wilcoxon p~6e-11). The signature survives strategic self-constructed deception (model invents its own lie, AUC 1.0), active concealment attempts (AUC 1.0), and length-controlled replication (20/20, AUC 1.0, p~1e-6). Using basis-free relative representations, a probe trained on one model family detects deception in two other families zero-shot (mean AUC 0.933), surviving simultaneous architecture and format change (AUC 0.821), and transfers across five languages (AUC 1.000, length-controlled). The signature is read-only: detectable but not injectable (0/8 both directions). Honest limitations and six negative experiments are documented in full.

</details>


### [148] [Uncertainty Quantification of Engineering Structures by Polynomial Chaos Expansion and Multivariate Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17233)
*Qitian Lu,Jafar Jafari-Asl,Panagiotis Spyridis,Lukas Novak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向多输出（向量值QoI）的自适应序贯采样方法，用于构建多项式混沌展开代理模型。该方法通过平衡输入空间的探索和所有输出聚合方差信息的利用，从候选池中序贯选择新样本。数值实验表明，相比非序贯拉丁超立方采样，该方法提高了代理模型的精度和稳定性，并提供了更可靠的二阶统计量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工程应用中，单一高保真模型在相同输入参数下产生多个感兴趣量（QoIs），不同输出对输入变量的敏感性不同。单一实验设计（ED）难以同时充分表示所有输出，而分别为每个输出单独采样会增加采样复杂度和计算成本，且忽略输出间相关性，损害数据一致性。

Method: 将基于多项式混沌展开（PCE）代理模型的自适应序贯采样方法推广至向量值QoIs。方法从候选池中序贯选择新样本，选择依据为样本对输出方差的局部贡献，同时平衡基于距离的输入空间探索（exploration）和跨所有输出聚合方差信息的利用（exploitation）。

Result: 通过多个工程问题的数值算例，将所提策略与非序贯拉丁超立方采样（LHS）进行对比。结果表明，所提策略在代理模型精度和稳定性上均有提升，并能提供更可靠的二阶统计量（方差等）估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应序贯采样方法能有效解决多输出场景下单一ED代表性不足的问题，在保持数据一致性的同时提升代理模型性能，为多QoI工程问题提供了一种更可靠的代理建模方案。

Abstract: In many engineering applications, a single high-fidelity model produces multiple quantities of interest (QoIs) under the same input parameters, e.g. finite element models of complex physical systems. To alleviate the high computational cost of direct model evaluations, surrogate models are widely used to construct efficient approximations of model responses. Naturally, the accuracy of surrogates strongly depends on the quality of the experimental design (ED). However, a single ED may not provide an adequate representation for all outputs simultaneously, especially when different outputs exhibit varying sensitivities to the input variables. A straightforward solution is to perform separate sampling for each output, but this results in increased sampling complexity and computational cost. From a statistical perspective, such an approach also ignores potential correlations among all outputs and may compromise data consistency. To address this issue, an adaptive sequential sampling method for constructing polynomial chaos expansion surrogate models is generalized for vector valued QoIs. The method sequentially selects new samples from a candidate pool based on their local contribution to the output variance, while balancing distance-based exploration of the input space and exploitation of aggregated variance information across all outputs. Its performance is compared with non-sequential Latin Hypercube Sampling through several numerical examples from engineering problems. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed strategy improves both surrogate accuracy and stability, and provides a more reliable estimation of second-order statistics.

</details>


### [149] [Rethinking Groups in Critic-Free RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17250)
*Yihong Wu,Liheng Ma,Lingfeng Xiao,Muzhi Li,Xinyu Wang,Yingxue Zhang,Jian-Yun Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出负性令牌过滤（Negative Token Filtering）策略，能够仅用单次轨迹（single-rollout）替代传统分组多次轨迹（group-based）进行强化学习训练，在保持推理任务性能的同时，在智能体任务上表现更强，解决了分组方法的数据效率低、同步障碍和灵活性差等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无评论家强化学习方法通常为同一个问题生成多组轨迹（group-based rollouts）来估计价值基线用于优势计算，但这种设计存在数据效率低下、组同步障碍以及与结构化轨迹不灵活等问题，因此需要更高效的训练策略。

Method: 重新审视"组"的作用，发现其核心功能并非估计基线，而是防止对负样本的错误惩罚。基于此洞察，提出负性令牌过滤（Negative Token Filtering）策略，实现稳定的单轨迹训练，并将其应用于两种批级别优势方法中。

Result: 在推理任务上取得了与分组强化学习技术相当的性能，在智能体任务上取得了更强的表现。

Conclusion: 通过揭示分组的核心作用是防止负样本误罚而非估计基线，提出了负性令牌过滤方法，实现了更灵活高效的单轨迹强化学习训练。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central paradigm for post-training large language models. Existing critic-free RL methods typically generate a group of rollouts for the same question to estimate value baselines for advantage computation. However, this design suffers from data inefficiency, group synchronization barriers, and inflexibility with structured rollouts. In this work, we revisit the role of the ``group'' and show that its underlying function is not merely to estimate baselines but to prevent false penalties on negative samples. Building on this insight, we propose negative token filtering, a simple and effective strategy that enables stable single-rollout training. We apply it to two batch-level advantage methods, achieving comparable performance on reasoning tasks and stronger performance on agentic tasks relative to group-based RL techniques.

</details>


### [150] [ProCUA-SFT Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17321)
*Jaehun Jung,Ximing Lu,Brandon Cui,Muhammad Khalifa,Shaokun Zhang,Hao Zhang,Jin Xu,Amala Sanjay Deshmukh,Karan Sapra,Andrew Tao,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz,Mingjie Liu,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ProCUA-SFT数据集（310万步级SFT样本），通过自动化流程从93000条合成轨迹中蒸馏得到，在UI-TARS 7B上微调一个epoch使OSWorld成功率从26.3%提升至45.0%，解决了AgentNet（22500条人类轨迹）导致负迁移的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最大公开数据集AgentNet在SFT中导致负迁移（OSWorld成功率从26.3%降至8-10%），需要大规模、多样化且有效的训练数据来训练计算机使用智能体（CUA）。

Method: 全自动化流水线：(i) 在真实桌面环境中合成基于实物的任务，使用SpreadsheetBench的912个电子表格、Zenodo10K的约1万个演示文稿以及OSWorld多应用配置；(ii) 通过二元前置条件检查验证任务可行性。单个VLM（Kimi-K2.5）同时作为目标生成器、前置条件判断器和轨迹执行器。每个轨迹扩展为步前缀样本以精确复现推理时的上下文布局。

Result: ProCUA-SFT包含310万步级SFT样本，来自2484个应用组合的93000条合成轨迹。微调UI-TARS 7B一个epoch后，OSWorld成功率达45.0%，比基线提升18.7个百分点，比AgentNet训练的模型高出35%以上。该数据子集还被用于Nemotron 3 Nano Omni模型的训练。

Conclusion: 大规模合成SFT数据可以有效解决人类轨迹数据的负迁移问题，显著提升CUA在桌面环境中的任务完成能力。

Abstract: Training computer-use agents (CUAs) -- models that interact with graphical desktops through screenshots and keyboard/mouse actions -- requires large-scale, diverse trajectory data collected in full desktop environments. The largest public resource, AgentNet (22.5K human trajectories), leads to negative transfer when used for supervised fine-tuning (SFT): continuing training UI-TARS 7B on AgentNet causes OSWorld success rate to fall from 26.3% to 8-10%. We present ProCUA-SFT, a dataset of 3.1M step-level SFT samples distilled from 93K synthetic trajectories across 2,484 application combinations. The dataset is produced by a fully automated pipeline that (i) synthesizes grounded tasks on live desktops seeded with real-world content -- 912 spreadsheets from SpreadsheetBench, approximately 10K permissively-licensed presentations from Zenodo10K, and multi-application OSWorld configs -- and (ii) verifies each task's feasibility through binary precondition checking before rollout. A single VLM (Kimi-K2.5) serves as goal generator, precondition judge, and trajectory executor, eliminating planner-actor capability gaps. Each trajectory is expanded into step-prefix samples that exactly reproduce the context layout seen at inference time. Fine-tuning UI-TARS 7B on ProCUA-SFT for one epoch yields 45.0% on OSWorld -- an 18.7 percentage-point improvement over the base model and over 35% above AgentNet-trained counterparts. A subset of ProCUA was incorporated into the training data for the Nemotron 3 Nano Omni model, contributing to its computer-use capabilities.

</details>


### [151] [Decision-Driven Geosteering Under Uncertainty: A Unified Framework for Sequential Decision Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17331)
*Hibat Errahmen Djecta,Sergey Alyaev,Kristian Fossum,Reidar B. Bratvold,Ressi Bonti Muhammad,Apoorv Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将粒子滤波（PF）与强化学习（RL）相结合的不确定性感知地质导向框架，在工业模拟器上验证了ADP、Deep Q-learning和Dual DRL三种决策策略，并引入了稳定性度量来评估钻井过程中的决策行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地质导向钻井需要在未知地质条件下进行顺序决策，传统方法缺乏对钻头前方地质不确定性的显式建模，亟需一种能够融合不确定性量化与序贯决策的框架。

Method: 采用粒子滤波进行概率性地下解释以显式表示地质不确定性，在此基础上耦合信念状态更新与三种决策策略：可解释的近似动态规划（ADP）、Deep Q-learning基线、以及基于目标Q网络和决斗架构的双深度强化学习（Dual DRL）。引入稳定性指标量化决策平滑度。

Result: 在工业地质导向模拟器中，在相同地质实现、操作限制和奖励定义下，对三种策略进行了受控、高保真的对比评估，超越了仅评估最终轨迹的传统方式。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够显式处理地质不确定性并指导序贯钻井决策，且稳定性度量提供了额外的操作洞察，有助于理解决策策略随不确定性演化的响应行为。

Abstract: Geosteering requires navigating a well trajectory through an unknown geological configuration, while sequentially updating decisions based on indirect measurements acquired during drilling. This work presents an uncertainty-aware geosteering framework that tightly integrates particle filtering for probabilistic subsurface interpretation with value-based reinforcement learning for sequential decision-making. Geological uncertainty ahead of the drill bit is represented explicitly through a particle filter (PF), enabling belief-informed control rather than deterministic trajectory correction.
  The framework couples PF belief updates with belief-informed decision policies and evaluates three decision-making options that operate under identical uncertainty representations: an interpretable Approximate Dynamic Programming (ADP) scheme, a Deep Q-learning baseline, and a Dual Deep Reinforcement Learning (Dual DRL) architecture trained with a target Q-network scheme for stability, using a dueling (value/advantage) decomposition for Q-value parameterization. Beyond final placement performance, we assess policy behavior using stability-oriented metrics that quantify steering smoothness over time, providing additional operational insight into how decision policies respond as uncertainty evolves.
  The framework is integrated with an API for validation within an industrial geosteering simulator under realistic measurement noise and drilling constraints. Using identical geological realizations, operational limits, and reward definitions across methods, the experiments provide a controlled and high-fidelity evaluation of how alternative decision policies behave throughout the drilling process, rather than evaluating performance solely from the final well trajectory.

</details>


### [152] [Counterfactual Optimization of Baseball Pitch Sequences and Estimation of Its Impact on Season-Level Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17345)
*Ryota Takamido,Hiroki Nakamoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用Transformer模型和反事实分析，发现优化投球序列中既有最终球也有准备球可显著提升投手赛季表现（如K/9提升超过1.0），并提供了速度区间有效位置、投球命令重要性等实用见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究主要关注单次打席中最后投球的优化，而对准备球的作用及其对赛季长期表现的影响关注不足。

Method: 使用MLB Statcast数据训练Transformer模型预测投球结果；通过反事实分析替换最终球或准备球的球种和位置，以最小化预测的击球概率；利用回归模型将模型输出与赛季统计数据关联。

Result: 优化最终球和准备球均可显著影响赛季水平表现，K/9提升超过1.0；提供了速度区间有效位置、投球命令重要性及中速球扩展选球选项等实用见解。

Conclusion: 研究定量支持了投球序列在棒球中的战略重要性。

Abstract: Although pitch sequencing is a central topic in baseball analytics, previous studies have primarily focused on optimizing the final pitch within a single plate appearance, leaving the role of preceding setup pitches and their impact on long-term season-level performance insufficiently examined. To address these issues, this study conducted counterfactual analyses using MLB Statcast data. A Transformer-based machine-learning model was trained to predict whether a target pitch would result in an in-play outcome or swing-out. Counterfactual pitch sequences were then generated by replacing either the final pitch or the preceding setup pitch with alternative pitch types and locations while keeping the surrounding contextual information fixed. Optimal counterfactual selections were defined as those that minimized the predicted in-play probability, and their expected effects on pitchers' seasonal statistics were estimated using regression models linking model outputs to season statistics. The results suggest that the optimization of both final and setup pitches may substantially influence season-level performance, including improvements of more than 1.0 in K/9. The analyses also provided several practical insights, including velocity-band-specific effective locations, the importance of pitch commands, and the expansion of pitch-selection options through middle-velocity pitches. These findings quantitatively support the strategic importance of pitch sequencing in baseball.

</details>


### [153] [MM++: Unsupervised Scale-Invariant Multilayer OOD Detection via Top-K Gated Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17352)
*Rahim Hossain,Md Tawheedul Islam Bhuian,Md Farhan Shadiq,Kyoung-Don Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MM++是一个完全无监督、严格事后且尺度不变的OOD检测框架，通过构建联合特征空间（融合熵密度下降筛选的中间层与终端表征，并采用Ledoit-Wolf正则化绑定协方差矩阵）来兼顾尺度不变性与层次表达性，无需辅助OOD数据、分类器微调或架构修改即可实现鲁棒的近端与远端OOD检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法在尺度不变性与层次表达性之间存在权衡，缺乏一种无需监督数据、无需模型修改即可同时兼顾两者的框架。

Method: 1) 通过测量熵密度下降识别区分性中间层；2) 将筛选后的中间层与终端表征融合以捕获跨层相关性并抑制早期层噪声；3) 采用Ledoit-Wolf正则化绑定协方差矩阵稳定统一特征空间。

Result: 在近端和远端OOD检测任务中，跨多种不同架构均取得稳健性能。

Conclusion: MM++通过联合特征空间与正则化协方差估计，有效解决了尺度不变性与层次表达性的矛盾，实现了无需额外数据或模型修改的高效OOD检测。

Abstract: We introduce MM++ (Multilayer Mahalanobis++), a fully unsupervised, strictly post-hoc, and scale-invariant framework for Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection. To address the trade-off between scale invariance and hierarchical expressivity, MM++ constructs a principled joint feature space. It first identifies discriminative intermediate layers by measuring entropy density drops, which mark the boundaries of sharp semantic compression. By fusing these selected layers with the terminal representation, the framework captures latent cross-layer correlations while mitigating early-layer noise. Crucially, a Ledoit-Wolf regularized tied covariance matrix stabilizes this unified space, enabling reliable distance estimation. Requiring no auxiliary OOD data, classifier fine-tuning, or architectural modifications, MM++ delivers robust performance across distinct architectures for both near- and far-OOD detection.

</details>


### [154] [Performance-Driven Environment Abstraction with Multi-Timescale Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17377)
*Yue Guan,Dipankar Maity,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究面向决策性能的大规模MDP环境抽象方法，通过状态空间聚合与共享动作分布构建受控近似，并提出一种多时间尺度强化学习框架来联合优化策略与树结构抽象，实验表明该方法能显著压缩状态空间、提升样本效率并加快重规划速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有环境抽象方法多关注几何或拓扑结构保持，而非直接优化决策质量，本文旨在设计一种直接以决策性能为导向的状态空间抽象方法。

Method: 将抽象建模为受控近似：通过聚合状态空间并强制每个聚合状态内共享动作分布；推导性能保证分离值函数近似误差与动作共享损失；基于此分析，设计多时间尺度强化学习框架，联合优化策略与树结构抽象，根据Q值差异细化或粗化状态空间区域。

Result: 实验表明该方法实现显著状态压缩、提升样本效率、加快重规划速度，优于演员-评论家基线。

Conclusion: 以决策性能为导向的环境抽象能够有效平衡抽象规模与决策质量，多时间尺度联合优化框架在实践中表现出色。

Abstract: We study performance-driven environment abstraction for decision-making in large Markov decision processes. Rather than preserving geometric or topological structure, we seek abstractions that directly optimize decision quality. We model abstraction as a controlled approximation obtained by aggregating the state space and enforcing a shared action distribution within each aggregated state. For a fixed partition, we establish a performance guarantee that separates value-function approximation error from the loss introduced by action sharing. Guided by this analysis, we develop a multi-timescale reinforcement learning framework that jointly adapts the policy and a tree-structured environment abstraction. The resulting algorithm refines and coarsens regions of the state space based on Q-value discrepancies, balancing performance against abstraction size and complexity. Empirical results demonstrate substantial state compression, improved sample efficiency, and faster replanning compared to actor-critic baselines.

</details>


### [155] [The Discrete-Log Clock: How a Transformer Learns Modular Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17399)
*Huu Danh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究小规模Transformer在模乘运算（a·b mod p）上的内部分布。先前工作认为嵌入层具有密集的傅里叶频谱，但本文指出这是分析基错误所致。使用乘法特征变换（基于乘法群(Z/pZ)^*的不可约表示）替代标准加法DFT，发现嵌入频谱变得高度稀疏（基尼系数从0.07提升至0.58），仅需4个关键频率。96.9%的MLP神经元仅响应单一乘法频率，且神经元激活热图按离散对数重排后呈现二维周期结构。这表明Transformer将模乘转化为离散对数空间的加法，实现了Nanda等人加法时钟算法的乘法版本——"离散对数时钟"算法。该方法具有普适性：匹配代数结构的分析基能揭示标准工具视为噪声的可解释结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究在分析Transformer学习模乘运算的内部表征时，使用标准加法DFT得到了密集的傅里叶频谱，与模加法中稀疏关键频率的现象形成鲜明对比。本文旨在解释这一矛盾，探究正确的分析基是否能让乘法任务的内部表征变得同样可解释。

Method: 作者使用乘法特征变换（乘法群(Z/pZ)^*的不可约表示对应的傅里叶变换）替代标准加法DFT，分析训练在a·b mod 113上的小Transformer的嵌入层和MLP神经元。通过计算基尼系数衡量频谱稀疏性，并观察神经元激活热图按离散对数重排后的周期性结构。

Result: 乘法基下嵌入频谱高度稀疏（基尼系数0.58 vs 加法基0.07），仅4个关键频率；96.9%的MLP神经元仅响应单一乘法频率；神经元热图按离散对数重排后呈现清晰的二维周期结构。

Conclusion: Transformer在内部将模乘运算转化为离散对数空间内的加法，实现了"离散对数时钟"算法。匹配数据代数结构的分析基能够揭示标准工具视为噪声的可解释结构，该方法可推广至其他代数结构的任务。

Abstract: When small transformers grok modular multiplication, prior work reports that the learned embedding has a "dense" Fourier spectrum requiring all frequencies. This contrasts with modular addition, where only a sparse set of key frequencies suffices. We show this density is an artifact of analyzing in the wrong basis. The natural Fourier transform for multiplication is not the standard additive DFT but the multiplicative character transform, which decomposes functions on the multiplicative group $(\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z})^*$ into its irreducible representations. Applying this transform to a grokked transformer trained on $a \cdot b \bmod 113$, we find the embedding spectrum becomes highly sparse (Gini coefficient 0.58 vs. 0.07 in the additive basis) with only 4 key frequencies carrying significant energy. Furthermore, 96.9% of MLP neurons are cleanly tuned to a single multiplicative frequency, and neuron activation heatmaps reveal 2D-periodic structure when reordered by the discrete logarithm. These results demonstrate the transformer reduces multiplication to addition in discrete-log space, implementing a "Discrete-Log Clock" algorithm analogous to Nanda et al.'s Clock algorithm for addition. The methodology generalizes: matching the analysis basis to the algebraic structure of the task reveals interpretable structure where standard tools see noise.

</details>


### [156] [Discrete Autoregressive Transformer for Generative Mechanism Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17409)
*Anar Nurizada,Anurag Purwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件自回归序列建模的平面路径综合方法，利用解码器-only Transformer和VAE潜变量生成机构关节坐标，在超过一百万个机构的语料库上实现了多拓扑类型的高精度路径匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面路径综合需要寻找机构使其耦合曲线匹配给定轨迹，而曲线到连杆机构的映射本质上是"一对多"的，涵盖四杆、六杆和八杆拓扑结构。现有方法难以在多种拓扑类型中高效生成多样且准确的机构方案。

Method: 将综合问题形式化为条件自回归序列建模：关节坐标经均匀量化后作为token，由解码器-only Transformer生成，输入包含目标曲线的VAE潜变量和显式机构类型token。训练采用token交叉熵损失与高斯平滑分箱辅助损失。推理时使用有界潜变量噪声调度，在每个噪声水平下解码所有机构类型，保留几何误差最小的前5个候选。

Result: 在保留测试集上，聚合平均Chamfer距离为0.0132，平均动态时间规整为0.153；基于训练集近邻潜变量的k近邻基线方法在匹配拓扑下平均Chamfer距离为0.0071，平均动态时间规整为0.117。

Conclusion: 该方法无需数据集查找即可生成多样且准确的机构族，在多种拓扑类型上均取得了优异的几何匹配精度。

Abstract: Planar path synthesis requires mechanisms whose coupler curves match a prescribed trajectory; the mapping from curve to linkage is inherently one-to-many across four-, six-, and eight-bar topologies. We address this design problem with simulation-grounded evaluation on a curated corpus of over one million mechanisms, reporting Chamfer distance and dynamic time warping after forward kinematics and geometric alignment. We formulate synthesis as conditional autoregressive sequence modeling: joint coordinates are uniformly quantized to tokens and generated by a decoder-only transformer with a variational-autoencoder (VAE) latent of the target curve and an explicit mechanism-type token. Training combines token cross-entropy with a Gaussian-smoothed bin auxiliary loss that respects ordinal structure among bins. At inference, a bounded latent-noise schedule decodes all mechanism types at each noise level; we retain the top five candidates by geometric error, yielding diverse accurate families without dataset lookup. On held-out tests, aggregate mean Chamfer distance is $0.0132$ and mean dynamic time warping is $0.153$; a latent $k$-nearest-neighbor baseline that conditions on training-set neighbor latents in VAE space achieves matched-topology mean Chamfer distance $0.0071$ and mean dynamic time warping $0.117$ using the same decoder.

</details>


### [157] [Amortized Probabilistic Retrieval of Atmospheric CO2 from OCO-2 Spectra Using Deep Learning with Laplace Approximations and Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17413)
*Alejandro Calle-Saldarriaga,Felix Jimenez,Jack Grosskreuz,Jiazheng Wang,Jonathan Hobbs,Matthias Katzfuss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的框架，用于替代NASA OCO-2卫星CO2浓度反演中计算昂贵的传统全物理算法，实现了快数量级的推理速度，并在点估计精度、不确定性量化及非高斯后验建模方面表现出显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCO-2卫星的CO2反演算法计算成本高且不能正确量化不确定性，亟需一种高效、准确且能提供可靠不确定性估计的替代方案。

Method: 采用多分支神经网络编码光谱波段，结合两种可扩展的不确定性量化方法（拉普拉斯近似和归一化流）估计CO2柱浓度的后验分布；在包含真实正向模型误差的高保真模拟数据集上训练。

Result: 相比传统全物理求解器，该方法在五个方面表现优越：①推理速度极大提升，支持实时处理；②对模型误差具有鲁棒性；③点估计精度更高；④不确定性校准更好；⑤能建模非高斯后验分布。

Conclusion: 基于仿真的深度学习是构建下一代业务化卫星遥感处理系统的可行路径。

Abstract: Space-based monitoring of atmospheric carbon dioxide (CO2) is essential for constraining the global carbon budget. NASA's Orbiting Carbon Observatory-2 (OCO-2) estimates column-averaged dry-air mole fractions of CO2 (XCO2) using high-resolution spectra. However, current operational retrieval algorithms are computationally expensive and do not properly quantify uncertainties. We present a novel deep learning framework that addresses these challenges. Due to the difficulties of ground-truth data for real satellite observations, we develop and validate our approach using a high-fidelity simulation dataset. This dataset, created to support OCO-2 uncertainty quantification (UQ), incorporates realistic forward model errors. Our architecture encodes spectral bands using a multi-branch neural network and estimates posteriors of the full CO2 column or desired summaries thereof using two scalable UQ methods: Laplace approximations and normalizing flows. Our approach has five key advantages relative to operational "full-physics" solvers: (1) Amortization: Inference is orders of magnitude faster, enabling real-time processing of massive data streams; (2) Model error robustness: By training on simulations that explicitly include model discrepancies, our method accounts for systematic errors often neglected by standard inversions; (3) Point estimate accuracy: We achieve superior predictive accuracy compared to baseline methods; (4) Improved UQ: The probabilistic outputs yield better-calibrated uncertainty estimates; and (5) Non-Gaussian posteriors: When utilizing normalizing flows, our framework successfully models complex, asymmetric posterior distributions, overcoming the limitations of the Gaussian assumption. These results suggest that simulation-based deep learning is a viable path toward next-generation operational processing systems.

</details>


### [158] [Memory-Efficient Meta-Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Safety-Critical Control in Adversarial Spacecraft Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17414)
*Alejandro Posadas-Nava,Richard Linares,Minduli Wijayatunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过元强化学习（meta-RL）调整输入约束控制屏障函数（ICCBF）中class-𝒦函数的最佳架构和训练算法，用于自主航天器交会与近距离操作（RPO）。对比了三种循环网络架构（LSTM、GRU、Mamba）和两种训练算法（PPO、SAC），结果表明Mamba结合PPO在所有合作与非合作场景下均实现了最优的任务完成率、安全性和燃油经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主航天器交会与近距离操作需要满足推力约束的同时保证安全并最小化燃料消耗。现有ICCBF方法通过meta-RL学习class-𝒦函数可提供鲁棒的非贪婪安全控制，但不同循环网络架构和训练算法组合的性能尚不明确，需系统比较以确定最优配置。

Method: 对比三种循环网络架构（LSTM、GRU、Mamba）和两种训练算法（PPO、SAC）的组合，通过meta-RL学习ICCBF中的class-𝒦函数。在合作场景及目标航天器存在对抗行为（恶化追踪器安全性）的非合作场景下评估性能。

Result: 状态空间模型Mamba结合PPO算法在所有合作与非合作测试场景中，相比其他架构和训练算法组合，在任务完成率、安全性和燃油节省方面均表现最优。

Conclusion: Mamba架构与PPO训练算法的组合是用于meta-RL调整ICCBF class-𝒦函数的最佳方案，适用于自主航天器交会与近距离操作中的安全关键控制。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) require controllers that guarantee safety under thrust constraints while minimizing fuel expenditure. Input-constrained control barrier functions (ICCBFs) provide a control method for nonlinear systems with actuation constraints that construct a forward-invariant safe set. Previous work has shown that learning class-$\mathcal{K}$ functions defining the ICCBF recursion via meta reinforcement learning (meta-RL) yields a robust, non-greedy approach to safety-critical control in RPO. This paper extends that framework further by investigating the performance of three recurrent network architectures (Long Short Term Memory (LSTM), Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), Selective State Space Model (Mamba)) and two training algorithms (Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Soft Actor Critic (SAC)) to identify the best setup for tuning ICCBF class-K functions via meta-RL. In addition to cooperative test cases, performance is evaluated in the presence of adversarial behavior where the target spacecraft behaves in a way that worsens the safety of the chaser spacecraft. Results indicate that state space models such as Mamba when used with PPO achieve superior task completion, safety, and fuel-savings compared to other architectures, across all cooperative and uncooperative scenarios tested.

</details>


### [159] [Generalization Guarantees for Multi-Input Neural Operator Learning in Sobolev Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17419)
*Yahong Yang,Zecheng Zhang,Wei Zhu,Wenjing Liao,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多输入神经算子，建立了在Sobolev范数下输出误差的逼近与泛化误差估计，允许输入函数定义在不同域、具有不同维度和Sobolev正则性，并显式量化了各输入空间对最终误差界的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子理论主要针对单输入函数设定，缺乏对多输入场景（如多源数据、多物理场耦合）的理论分析框架，尤其缺乏在Sobolev范数下的误差界估计。

Method: 通过理论推导建立了多输入神经算子的逼近误差与泛化误差界，显式刻画了各输入空间的维度、正则性和Sobolev阶数对最终误差的贡献，模型复杂度依赖保留log log/log型结构。

Result: 在平衡情形下，逼近与泛化速率由输入维度、正则性和Sobolev阶数之间的相互作用主导；误差界显式量化了每个输入空间的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作为多输入算子学习（含Sobolev训练）提供了通用理论框架，可应用于偏微分方程和科学计算中的算子学习问题。

Abstract: We develop approximation and generalization error estimates for multi-input neural operators, with the output error measured in Sobolev norms. In contrast to standard operator-learning settings with a single input function, our framework allows multiple input functions defined on possibly different domains, with different dimensions and Sobolev regularities. The derived rates explicitly quantify the contribution of each input space to the final error bound. In particular, in the balanced regime, the approximation and generalization rates are governed by the interaction between the input dimensions, regularities, and Sobolev orders, while the dependence on the model complexity retains a \(\log\log/\log\)-type structure. Our analysis provides a general theoretical framework for multi-input operator learning, including Sobolev training, and is applicable to operator learning problems arising from partial differential equations and scientific computing.

</details>


### [160] [MorphStrata: Layer-Specific Perturbations for Generating Morphence Students in Time-Series Moving Target Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17435)
*Abhishek Bhardwaj,Arnav Doshi,Anusri Nagarajan,Thanh Quynh Nhu Ta,Mohammad Masum,Robert Chun,Jaydip Sen,Saptarshi Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MorphStrata是一种基于选择性、逐层随机噪声注入的学生模型生成策略，通过Transformer教师网络创建结构异质性的学生集成，在边际训练成本下显著提升对抗鲁棒性，在AEP数据集上FGSM和BIM攻击下RMSE分别降低24.11%和97.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测模型易受基于梯度的对抗攻击，现有防御机制（尤其是移动目标防御MTD）在鲁棒性、响应受限性和计算成本之间存在权衡，维持多个随机模型实例会显著增加训练开销。

Method: 提出MorphStrata，以Transformer为教师网络，对随机选择的架构块注入逐层随机噪声，生成结构异质性的学生模型集成；扩展传统Morphence防御框架，实现选择性架构扰动。

Result: 在Jena Climate、Electricity Load Diagrams、Appliances Energy Prediction等基准上，使用FGSM、BIM和PGD攻击评估。MorphStrata在多个攻击强度和数据集上保持可比较的对抗RMSE；在AEP高熵周期性数据上表现最优，FGSM下RMSE提升24.11%，BIM下提升97.97%（ε=0.5，30次随机试验）。训练时间增长低于Morphence MTD基线的1%，同时实现两位数的对抗RMSE降低。学生模型间L2距离与防御效果正相关。

Conclusion: MorphStrata以边际成本增量维持了MTD防御的对抗鲁棒性，优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting models remain vulnerable to gradient-based adversarial attacks while existing defense mechanisms typically incur a trade-off in robustness for bounded response and compute cost. The problem is pronounced in Moving Target Defense where maintaining multiple randomized model instances substantially exacerbates the training overhead. In this work, we introduce MorphStrata, a student generation strategy with selective, layer-specific stochastic noise injection that extends the traditional Morphence defense. MorphStrata uses a Transformer backbone as the teacher and perturbs randomly selected architectural blocks to create structured heterogeneity across student models in response to varied data distributions and threat models. We evaluate against vanilla Transformer and Morphence backbones on a suite of benchmarks including the Jena Climate, Electricity Load Diagrams, and Appliances Energy Prediction using FGSM, BIM and PGD attacks across multiple attack strengths. Across datasets and attack regimes, the proposed ensemble maintains comparable adversarial RMSE. Specifically, for high entropy, periodic datasets as in the case of the AEP data, MorphStrata achieves the lowest RMSE across all attacks and perturbation budgets, improving over the static baseline by up to 24.11% and 97.97% under FGSM and BIM respectively at an epsilon value of 0.5 over 30 randomized trials. Targeting the layers to generate MorphStrata students accounts for less than 1% increase in train-times over the Morphence MTD baseline for most of the experiments, while accounting for double digit gains in adversarial RMSE reduction. We also observe a positive correlation between higher pairwise L2 distance (among generated students) and overall defense effectiveness. In summary, MorphStrata maintains adversarial robustness as an MTD defense at marginal cost deltas when compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [161] [Toward Controllable Catalyst Inverse Design via Large-Scale Autoregressive Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17445)
*Dong Hyeon Mok,Jonggeol Na,Seoin Back*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GPT架构的条件催化剂生成模型，通过数值嵌入层实现分类和连续属性的条件控制。模型在1.33亿催化剂结构上预训练，并在约46万优化结构上微调，实现了98%的结构有效性、95%的优化有效性及93%的吸附质-组成联合匹配率。在结合能条件生成中，匹配率较基线提升4倍，筛选效率提升1.5-4倍，展示了大规模自回归预训练结合显式属性条件控制在可控催化剂生成中的实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非均相催化剂表面结构复杂，表面-吸附质耦合作用广泛，传统筛难以高效探索庞大的化学空间。虽然基于机器学习的高通量筛选加速了催化剂发现，但搜索空间增大时效率下降，因此需要开发能直接构建具有目标属性的催化剂的生成模型。

Method: 采用GPT架构，引入数值嵌入层，在统一的自回归框架内同时处理分类属性（如吸附质类型、组成）和连续属性（如结合能）的条件生成。模型分为两阶段：先在1.33亿催化剂结构上预训练，再在约46万优化结构上微调，微调数据包含分类属性和结合能信息。

Result: 模型结构有效性98%，优化有效性95%，分类条件保真度高，吸附质类型与组成的联合匹配率达93%。结合能条件生成匹配率约20%，较基线分布提升4倍；生成分布系统性地向目标值偏移，无需额外微调即可使反应靶向催化剂发现的筛选效率提升1.5-4倍。

Conclusion: 大规模自回归预训练结合显式属性条件控制，为实现可控催化剂生成和加速催化剂发现提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Inverse design of heterogeneous catalysts remains challenging because catalyst surfaces exhibit substantial structural complexity with coupled surface-adsorbate interactions across a vast chemical space that is difficult to explore efficiently through conventional screening alone. Although machine learning-based high-throughput screening has accelerated catalyst discovery, its efficiency inevitably declines as the search space grows, motivating the development of generative models that can directly construct catalysts with target properties. Here, we present a conditional catalyst generative model based on the Generative Pretrained Transformer architecture with a numerical embedding layer that enables the generation of catalyst structures conditioned on both categorical and continuous properties within a single autoregressive framework. The model was pretrained on 133 million catalyst structures and subsequently fine-tuned on approximately 460,000 optimized structures with associated categorical properties and binding energies for conditional generation. The resulting model achieved 98% structural validity, 95% optimization validity, and high categorical condition fidelity, with a 93 % joint match rate for adsorbate type and composition. For binding energy conditioning, the match rate of approximately 20% represents a four-fold improvement over the baseline training distribution, and the generated distributions shift systematically toward the target values, enabling a 1.5 to 4-fold improvement in screening efficiency for reaction-targeted catalyst discovery without additional fine-tuning. These results show that large-scale autoregressive pre-training, combined with explicit property conditioning, provides a practical route toward controllable catalyst generation and accelerated catalysts discovery.

</details>


### [162] [Credibility-Weighted Pricing of Autonomous Vehicle Liability Under Operational Design Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17451)
*Doyeon Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分层贝叶斯可信度框架，通过ODD相似性核函数聚合多城市、多软件版本数据，解决自动驾驶系统部署中的费率制定难题。基于Waymo实际碰撞数据验证，部分池化方法显著优于无池化方法，且学习到的核函数优势在约12个部署城市时变得可检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统部署面临三个核心费率制定挑战：稀疏的驾驶经验数据、不断变化的运行设计领域(ODD)以及跨软件版本的非平稳风险，传统精算方法难以适用。

Method: 构建分层贝叶斯可信度框架，通过学习的ODD相似性核函数在多个城市、软件版本和区域间进行信息池化，以Buhlmann-Straub模型作为特例。使用NHTSA Standing General Order数据库中Waymo在四个美国都市区的648起已验证碰撞事件与1.16亿匹配里程进行验证。

Result: 城市聚合可信度权重为0.12-0.46（中等水平），部分池化方法显著优于无池化方法。功效分析表明，学习到的核函数优势在约12个部署城市时变得可检测。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层贝叶斯框架能有效利用稀疏的自动驾驶数据实现更准确的费率制定，ODD相似性核函数在跨城市池化中展现出明显优势，为自动驾驶保险精算提供了可行方法论。

Abstract: Automated Driving System deployments create a foundational ratemaking challenge: sparse experience, shifting operational design domains, and non-stationary risk across software releases. We propose a hierarchical Bayesian credibility framework pooling across cities, software versions, and territories via a learned ODD-similarity kernel, nesting Buhlmann-Straub as a limiting case. Demonstrated on 648 verified-engaged Waymo crashes across four U.S. metros from the NHTSA Standing General Order database against 116 million matched miles, city-aggregate credibility weights are moderate (0.12-0.46), partial pooling decisively outperforms no pooling, and a power analysis shows the learned kernel's advantage becomes detectable at approximately twelve deployed cities.

</details>


### [163] [Operator Boosting Produces Pareto-Efficient PDE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17460)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Operator Boosting——一种逐阶段残差学习框架，通过训练一系列小型同族神经算子来直接构建紧凑的PDE代理模型，无需训练大模型后再压缩。在30个数据集-架构对中，该方法减少72-95%的可训练参数，并在21个案例中带来正的平均精度提升，在7/10的PDE基准上实现了经验帕累托改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全尺寸神经算子模型在多查询科学工作流中存储、部署和评估成本高昂，现有方法通常先训练大模型再压缩，效率低下。本文旨在直接构建紧凑的神经算子代理模型，在保持精度的同时大幅降低参数量。

Method: 提出Operator Boosting框架，从归一化输出坐标中的经验均值预测器开始，依次在残差场上训练一系列小型同族神经算子（FNO、DeepONet、CNO），并通过验证集选择的收缩因子整合每次修正。采用逐阶段残差学习策略，直接构造紧凑代理而非后压缩。

Result: 在30个数据集-架构对中，21个展示正的平均精度提升，17个具有正的置信区间；所有提升后的堆叠模型参数减少约72-95%。在10个PDE基准中的7个（包括二维Navier-Stokes、浅水动力学、达西流、一维输运和反应系统、三维可压缩Navier-Stokes）上实现经验帕累托改进。

Conclusion: Operator Boosting通常能改善神经PDE代理的精度-参数帕累托前沿，但也揭示了在某些PDE和架构依赖的场景下残差提升无法抵消压缩带来的性能损失。

Abstract: Neural operators are widely used as surrogate solution maps for partial differential equations (PDEs), but full-size models can be costly to store, deploy, and evaluate in many-query scientific workflows. This work introduces Operator Boosting, a stagewise residual-learning framework for constructing compact neural-operator surrogates directly, rather than training a large model and compressing it afterward. Starting from the empirical mean predictor in normalized output coordinates, the method trains a sequence of tiny same-family neural operators on residual fields and incorporates each correction through validation-selected shrinkage. We instantiate the framework with Fourier neural operators (FNOs), DeepONets, and convolutional neural operators (CNOs), and compare boosted tiny stacks against full-size monolithic baselines across one-, two-, and three-dimensional PDE benchmarks from PDEBench, APEBench, and The Well. Across 30 dataset-architecture pairs, 21 show positive mean accuracy gains and 17 have positive confidence intervals, while all boosted stacks reduce trainable parameter count by approximately 72-95%. Best-model comparisons show empirical Pareto improvements on 7 of 10 completed PDE benchmarks, including two-dimensional Navier-Stokes, shallow-water dynamics, Darcy flow, one-dimensional transport and reaction systems, and three-dimensional compressible Navier-Stokes. These results show that Operator Boosting often improves the empirical accuracy-parameter Pareto frontier of neural PDE surrogates, while also exposing PDE- and architecture-dependent regimes where residual boosting fails to offset compression.

</details>


### [164] [ResAware: Cross-Environment Website Fingerprinting via Resource-Privileged Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17462)
*Chongru Fan,Wei Wang,Wentao Huang,Zhenquan Ding,Jinqiao Shi,Lei Cui,Zhiyu Hao,Xiaochun Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ResAware是一种跨环境资源感知蒸馏框架，通过教师模型学习资源级特征，将特权知识蒸馏给学生模型，使学生模型仅使用加密流量进行推理，显著提升了网站指纹攻击在时空漂移、浏览器异构等真实环境下的鲁棒性。在五个月收集的16万+样本上，将Var-CNN在150天时间漂移下的F1分数从72.77%提升至81.49%，开放世界TPR@1%FPR从22.40%提升至27.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网站指纹攻击在实验室环境下表现良好，但在真实环境中由于时空漂移、浏览器异构、代理混淆等因素导致性能严重下降，根本原因在于它们仅依赖低层次流量特征，这些特征噪声大且对环境扰动高度敏感。

Method: 提出ResAware框架，采用"训练丰富/推理贫乏"的非对称设置：教师模型在资源级特征上训练，通过异构知识蒸馏将特权知识传递给学生模型；学生模型在部署时仅使用加密流量进行推理，零额外开销。

Result: 在五个月从6个全球分布观测点收集的16万+配对样本上评估，ResAware显著增强了多种WF基线的跨环境鲁棒性。150天时间漂移下，Var-CNN的F1分数从72.77%提升至81.49%，开放世界TPR@1%FPR从22.40%提升至27.20%。

Conclusion: 资源级监督能够在不扩展在线观测能力的前提下提升网站指纹攻击的鲁棒性，验证了利用资源层特权知识进行蒸馏的有效性。

Abstract: While Website Fingerprinting (WF) attacks achieve high accuracy in controlled laboratory settings, they often degrade substantially in real-world environments due to spatio-temporal drift, browser heterogeneity, proxy obfuscation and etc. This limitation stems from their sole reliance on low-level traffic features that are noisy and highly sensitive to environmental perturbations. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{ResAware}, a cross-environment resource-aware distillation framework under a \textit{training-rich/inference-poor} asymmetric setting. Specifically, ResAware trains a teacher model on resource-level features, and then distills the resulting privileged knowledge into a student model through heterogeneous knowledge distillation. At deployment time, the student model performs inference using only encrypted traffic, incurring zero additional cost. We evaluate ResAware on a large-scale dataset collected over five months from six globally distributed vantage points, comprising more than $160{,}000$ paired samples. The results show that ResAware significantly enhances the cross-environment robustness of diverse WF baselines. Under a 150-day temporal drift, for example, ResAware improves the F1-score of Var-CNN from $72.77\%$ to $81.49\%$ and the open-world $TPR@1\%FPR$ from $22.40\%$ to $27.20\%$. Our results demonstrate that resource-level supervision improves WF robustness without expanding online observation capabilities.

</details>


### [165] [CheckMIABench: Firm Foundations For Membership Inference Attacks on Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17464)
*Jeffrey G. Wang,Jason Wang,Marvin Li,Seth Neel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对语言模型的成员推断攻击（MIA）评估中存在的分布偏移问题，提出了一种基于固定训练时间点前后数据同分布特性的基准测试框架，并在Pythia和OLMo模型家族（70M到7B参数）上验证了多种攻击方法，同时开源了模块化攻击库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIA评估存在成员与非成员集之间的微妙分布偏移，导致统计有效性受损，甚至“盲”方法表现优于已发布攻击方法，亟需构建更严谨的评估基准。

Method: 利用训练过程中固定时间点前后数据同分布的特性，将所有具有中间检查点和公开训练数据的开源模型转化为MIA测试平台，并构建了模块化攻击库。

Result: 在Pythia和OLMo模型家族（70M到7B参数）上对半打已发布攻击方法进行了评估，并开源了库。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了更可靠的LLM隐私评估基准，促进了隐私研究的可复现性和标准化。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are a canonical way to assess a machine learning model's privacy properties. Although several attempts have been made to evaluate MIAs on language models, the extant literature has suffered numerous difficulties in constructing clean evaluations to test new techniques. In particular, subtle distribution shifts between member and non-member sets can undermine the statistical validity of MIAs; recent work has underscored this by showing that "blind" methods with no access to the underlying model can perform far better than published methods on the same benchmarks. This paper constructs a benchmark for principled evaluation of MIAs against LLMs, by leveraging the insight that training data before and after a fixed point during training are drawn from the same distribution. Therefore, all open-source models with intermediate checkpoints and public training data can be converted into MIA testbeds. We apply our framework to a half-dozen published attacks on the Pythia and OLMo family of models, from 70M to 7B parameters. To facilitate further privacy research, we open-source a modular library for designing and implementing attacks in this setting: https://github.com/safr-ai-lab/pandora_llm.

</details>


### [166] [Perron--Frobenius Operator Matching for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17465)
*Shiqi Zhang,Wuwei Wu,Jaemin Oh,Jie Chen,Xiaoning Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Perron-Frobenius算子匹配（PFOM）框架，通过积分PF算子统一流、扩散和跳跃生成模型，证明KL散度在Bregman散度中唯一能保持密度级与样本条件目标的一致性，并开发Nesterov加速训练与采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（流、扩散、跳跃）各成体系，缺乏统一的理论框架；同时训练和采样的数值稳定性与收敛速度有待提升。

Method: 1) 利用Perron-Frobenius算子匹配密度演化，统一流/扩散/跳跃模型；2) 理论证明KL散度是Bregman散度中唯一保持密度级与样本条件目标等价的散度，导出与Koopman路径匹配等价的实用损失；3) 引入Nesterov加速方法稳定离散化并加速收敛。

Result: 在Gaussian混合和two-moons数据集上，PFOM实现更快的KL/W2/MMD下降以及更优的壁钟效率。

Conclusion: PFOM将算子理论识别与现代生成建模统一，为自适应字典和高维应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce Perron--Frobenius Operator Matching (PFOM), a generative framework that matches density evolution via the integral PF operator, subsuming flow, diffusion, and jump models. We prove that among Bregman divergences, only Kullback--Leibler divergence preserves equality between density-level and sample-conditioned objectives, yielding a practical loss equivalent to Koopman path matching. We further develop Nesterov-accelerated training and sampling that stabilize discretization and accelerate convergence. %On Gaussian mixtures and two-moons, PFOM achieves faster KL/$W_2$/MMD decrease and improved wall-clock efficiency with empirical validation. PFOM unifies operator-theoretic identification with modern generative modeling and opens paths to adaptive dictionaries and high-dimensional applications.

</details>


### [167] [ReRAM-aware Model Finetuning addressing I-V Non-linearity and Retention Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17471)
*Ching-Yi Lin,Shamik Kundu,Arnab Raha,Sahil Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于微调的硬件感知训练算法，通过范围收缩的sinh变换缓解I-V非线性，并将保留误差作为正则化损失纳入微调过程，以极小的训练开销实现深度学习模型在ReRAM上的鲁棒部署。在图像分类（ResNet18、DeiT-Tiny、MobileNetV3）和问答（SQuAD v2）任务上，精度退化控制在2%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CPU/GPU/NPU架构受限于冯·诺依曼瓶颈，而基于ReRAM交叉阵列的存内计算（IMC）虽具有高密度、高能效的优势，但其非理想特性限制了实际部署。现有硬件感知训练框架通常需要从头训练，对于现代大规模模型而言计算开销过高。

Method: 提出一种基于微调的硬件感知训练算法：1) 采用范围收缩的sinh（range-shrunk sinh）变换来缓解I-V非线性问题；2) 将保留误差（retention errors）直接作为正则化损失项纳入微调过程。

Result: 在ResNet18和DeiT-Tiny等大规模模型上达到与基准模型相近的准确率；在ImageNet上MobileNetV3系列精度退化低于2%；在SQuAD v2数据集上F-1分数仅下降1个点。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够以极小的训练开销实现DNN在ReRAM上的鲁棒部署，有效解决了硬件非理想性带来的精度退化问题。

Abstract: Traditional CPU, GPU, and NPU architectures are increasingly limited by the von Neumann bottleneck. While In-Memory Computing (IMC) using ReRAM crossbar arrays offers a high-density, energy-efficient alternative, its practical deployment is constrained through their non-idealities. Existing hardware-aware training frameworks often require training from scratch, which is computationally prohibitive for modern large-scale models. In this work, we propose a finetuning-based hardware-aware training algorithm that enables robust DNN deployment on ReRAM with minimal training overhead. Our approach mitigates I-V non-linearity by applying a range-shrunk sinh transformation and incorporates retention errors directly into a regularization loss during the finetuning process. We evaluate our framework across models and tasks such as image classification and question-answering (QA). Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves similar accuracy on large-scale models like ResNet18 and DeiT-Tiny as the base model. In-case of ImageNet for MobileNetV3 families the technique has only less than 2% accuracy degradation. Further, applying the technique on the SQuAD v2 dataset results in only 1 point degradation of F-1 score.

</details>


### [168] [Multi-Adapter PPO: A Cross-Attention Enhanced Wavelength Selection Framework for LIBS Quantitative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17476)
*Hao Li,Man Fung Zhuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多适配器PPO强化学习框架的波长选择方法，用于LIBS定量分析，在钢铁和煤炭数据集上相比PSO综合评分提升28.4%，预测精度提升45.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIBS定量分析中高维光谱数据带来的波长选择难题，以及预测精度与特征效率之间的根本性权衡问题。

Method: 将波长选择转化为强化学习问题，提出Multi-Adapter PPO框架，利用交叉注意力机制和多个专门适配器捕捉复杂光谱关系。

Result: 在钢铁和煤炭数据上，相比PSO综合评分平均提升28.4%，预测精度平均提升45.2%。

Conclusion: 该方法在平衡预测精度与特征效率方面表现优越，达到了LIBS定量分析的最新水平，同时保持了可解释性和计算效率。

Abstract: Laser-induced breakdown spectroscopy (LIBS) quantitative analysis faces critical challenges in wavelength selection due to high-dimensional spectral data and the fundamental trade-off between prediction accuracy and feature efficiency. This paper presents a novel Multi-Adapter PPO framework that transforms wavelength selection into a reinforcement learning problem, leveraging cross-attention mechanisms and multiple specialized adapters to capture complex spectral relationships. Our approach outperforms traditional Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) by an average of 28.4\% in comprehensive score and 45.2\% in prediction accuracy across steel and coal datasets. The proposed method demonstrates superior performance in balancing prediction accuracy with feature efficiency, achieving state-of-the-art results in LIBS quantitative analysis while maintaining interpretability and computational efficiency. We released our code and dataset here: https://github.com/Hflying/MAPPO

</details>


### [169] [Online LLM Selection via Constrained Bandits with Time-Varying Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17489)
*Yin Huang,Qingsong Liu,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对边缘-云推理系统中大语言模型在线选择问题，提出了一个受约束的随机赌博机学习框架及相应算法，在满足硬性资源预算和软性服务质量约束的同时，实现了次线性遗憾和次线性约束违反的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型部署中，面对模型异构性、性能特征未知且随机、任务需求时变等挑战，静态选择策略无法满足服务质量与资源利用效率的要求，亟需一种能在硬性资源预算和软性服务等级约束下进行在线决策的自适应方法。

Method: 将问题建模为受约束的随机赌博机学习任务，同时处理packing型（硬约束）和covering型（软约束）两种约束，并适应时变任务需求。提出一种基于置信区间估计和需求预测的新型在线学习算法，无需事先知道奖励、成本或延迟分布，仅依赖部分反馈进行决策。

Result: 理论上证明了算法相对于完全信息的离线基准具有次线性遗憾和次线性covering约束违反；在合成工作负载上的实验验证了该方法在动态、资源受限环境中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM边缘-云推理系统中的模型在线选择提供了理论基础和实用算法，能在复杂约束下自适应平衡奖励最大化与长期约束满足。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in edge-cloud inference systems to handle diverse user tasks with heterogeneous accuracy, latency, and cost profiles. Selecting the appropriate LLM for each incoming task is critical for ensuring service quality and efficient resource utilization. However, model heterogeneity, stochastic and unknown performance characteristics, and time-varying task demands make static selection strategies inadequate. Real-world deployments often impose hard resource budgets such as monetary expenditure limits, along with soft service-level requirements such as latency guarantees. These constraints introduce additional challenges for online decision-making. We formulate this problem as a constrained stochastic bandit learning task, where the learner sequentially selects models under both packing-type (hard) and covering-type (soft) constraints, while adapting to time-varying task demand. The learner operates without access to the underlying reward, cost, or latency distributions and must rely on partial feedback. We develop a novel online learning algorithm that leverages confidence-bound estimates and demand predictions to balance reward maximization with long-term constraint satisfaction. We provide theoretical guarantees showing sublinear regret and sublinear covering constraint violations compared to an offline benchmark with full information. Experimental results on synthetic workloads demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our approach in dynamic, resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [170] [Reconfigurable Computing Challenge: Transformer for Jet Tagging on Versal AI Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17500)
*Gram Koski,Sean Lipps,Zhenghua Ma,G. Abarajithan,Ryan Kastner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复用的软件框架，将量化的整数-only Transformer模型映射到AMD Versal AI Engine上用于喷注标记，并自动从高层Python描述生成Vitis图代码，作为开源软件发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在CERN LHC的喷注标记中表现优异，但在低延迟、资源受限的触发系统中部署面临挑战，亟需高效的硬件部署方案。

Method: 将密集层和多头注意力层映射到AIE tiles，构建可组合的AIE构建块表示Transformer层，并开发自动从Python模型生成Vitis图代码的框架。

Result: 在AMD Versal AIE上实现了量化的整数-only Transformer喷注标记器初始版本，并开源了软件框架。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来研究提供了基础，并作为开源软件发布以推动相关领域发展。

Abstract: Transformer-based models achieve strong performance for jet tagging at the CERN LHC, but deploying them in low-latency, resource-constrained trigger systems is challenging. We present an initial implementation of a quantized, integer-only transformer for jet tagging on the AMD Versal AI Engine (AIE), mapping dense and multi-head attention (MHA) layers to AIE tiles. The main contribution is a reusable software framework that represents transformer layers as composable AIE building blocks and automatically generates the corresponding Vitis graph code from a high-level Python model description. This framework provides a foundation for future research and is released as open-source software at https://github.com/KastnerRG/particle_transformer_aie.

</details>


### [171] [When the Next Step Is Not One Step: Distribution-Aware Execution Modeling for Concurrent Go Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17508)
*Kaviru Hapuarachchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对并发程序非确定性调度导致的预测难题，本文通过将多次运行结果聚合为经验分布，使用KL散度微调7B模型，在798个真实Go缺陷预测中达到36.2%准确率，并形式化推导了goroutine泄露的调度语义签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发程序的非确定性调度使得相同前缀可能产生多个合法后继事件，传统单标签预测模型本质上是在猜测随机过程的一个结果，学习信号存在根本性缺陷。

Method: 对同一程序多次运行，将观测到的后继事件聚合为经验分布；使用KL散度作为训练目标微调7B参数模型，使其输出分布匹配经验分布。

Result: 在798个来自CockroachDB、Kubernetes、gRPC、etcd等真实生产Go缺陷的预测任务上，微调后准确率36.2%，超过Gemini 3.5 Flash零样本的34.8%和未微调模型的28.6%；分布训练与交叉熵准确率相当(35.8% vs 36.2%)，但期望校准误差从0.205降至0.169。

Conclusion: 利用非确定性作为训练信号是有效的；分布训练在保持准确率的同时显著提升了校准度；可形式化推导goroutine泄露的调度语义签名。

Abstract: Training a model to predict the next step in a concurrent program is harder than it looks: two runs of the same program from the same trace prefix can produce different next events, both valid, because the scheduler is nondeterministic. A model trained against a single label is learning to guess one outcome of a random process. We turn this around and use the nondeterminism as a training signal. We run each program many times, aggregate the observed next events into an empirical distribution, and fine-tune a 7B model to match that distribution with a KL objective. On 798 held-out predictions drawn from real production Go bugs (CockroachDB, Kubernetes, gRPC, etcd), fine-tuning on fewer than a thousand traces reaches 36.2% accuracy, ahead of Gemini 3.5 Flash used zero-shot (34.8%) and the same model without fine-tuning (28.6%). Distribution training matches cross-entropy on accuracy (35.8% vs. 36.2%) while reducing Expected Calibration Error from 0.205 to 0.169. We also derive a formal goroutine-leak signature for a class of select-blocked goroutines where P(GoUnblock)=0 holds by scheduler semantics, not by learning. We release the dataset, trained adapters, and all tooling.

</details>


### [172] [Geometry-Aware Post-Hoc Uncertainty Quantification in Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17513)
*Oriol Vendrell-Gallart,Nima Negarandeh,Ramin Bostanabad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REEF-GP是一个后验不确定性量化框架，通过在冻结的神经算子残差上拟合高斯过程，利用算子内在的坐标-特征表示构建几何感知的不确定性，在五个PDE基准上达到与深度集成相当的校准不确定性，但计算成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子的不确定性量化主要关注网络参数的不确定性，忽略了算子自身学习的几何感知表示。需要一种能够利用几何感知特征空间进行高效、可扩展的不确定性量化的方法。

Method: 提出REEF-GP框架，在冻结神经算子的残差上拟合高斯过程，其核函数由算子的内部嵌入定义。采用谱归一化投影、异方差几何感知噪声和高效的子集训练，避免低秩近似，确保非结构域上的稳定性和可扩展性。

Result: 在五个不同几何结构的PDE基准上，REEF-GP保持了预测精度，实现了与深度集成竞争但成本极低的校准不确定性估计，在几何分布偏移下鲁棒，不确定性集中在物理有意义区域（如激波前沿）。

Conclusion: 准确且可扩展的神经算子后验不确定性量化可以直接在其学习的特征空间中实现，为参数中心方法提供了实用替代方案。

Abstract: Neural operators provide fast surrogates for PDEs but their deterministic predictions limit their use in tasks requiring uncertainty quantification (UQ), especially under geometric variability. Existing approaches primarily model uncertainty in network parameters, largely overlooking the geometry-aware representations learned by the operator itself. We propose REEF-GP (Residual on Embedded Features Gaussian Process), a post-hoc UQ framework that fits a GP to the residuals of a frozen neural operator whose internal embeddings define the kernel feature space. Rather than learning a separate feature map, REEF-GP adapts the operator's intrinsic coordinate-feature representations to construct geometry-aware uncertainties. To ensure stability and scalability on unstructured domains, REEF-GP incorporates spectral-normalized projections, heteroscedastic geometry-aware noise, and efficient subset-based training that avoids restrictive low-rank approximations. Across five PDE benchmarks with varying geometries, REEF-GP preserves predictive accuracy while achieving calibrated uncertainty estimates competitive with deep ensembles but at a fraction of their cost. Our approach remains robust under geometric distribution shift, with uncertainty concentrating in physically meaningful regions (e.g., shock fronts). Our results demonstrate that accurate and scalable post-hoc UQ for neural operators can be achieved directly in their learned feature space, offering a practical alternative to parameter-centric approaches.

</details>


### [173] [FoundCause: Causal Discovery with Latent Confounders from Observational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17516)
*Patrick Blöbaum,Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FoundCause，一种基于合成数据训练的可迁移因果发现模型，通过单次前向传播直接从观测数据推断因果图，首次在可迁移因果发现中显式建模隐变量混淆，在15个真实数据集上超越11种经典方法和4种可迁移方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的因果发现方法（如PC、GES、NOTEARS）通常需要迭代优化且难以处理隐混淆变量，而现有的可迁移因果发现方法也未能显式建模隐混淆。作者旨在开发一种能够从观测数据中高效、准确地恢复因果结构并处理隐混淆和缺失数据的端到端模型。

Method: FoundCause采用置换不变的Transformer编码器，通过交替注意力机制在样本和变量两个维度上建模跨变量依赖和单变量分布；利用经典非对称性度量派生出的成对统计特征注入注意力机制；因子化解码器分离边存在性与方向性；三角细化模块推理链式、碰撞器等高阶因果结构；基于可学习隐编码的混淆模块显式建模隐变量共同原因；通过掩码输入表示处理缺失数据。整个模型在合成结构因果模型数据上训练。

Result: 在15个真实数据集上，FoundCause相比最优非可迁移方法，F1提升9.6%，AUROC提升1.2%，结构汉明距离降低18.9%；同时优于4种可迁移因果发现方法，且推理仅需单次前向传播。

Conclusion: FoundCause是首个显式建模隐混淆的可迁移因果发现方法，证明了基于合成数据训练的模型能够有效迁移到真实因果发现任务中，并在效率（单次前向传播）和效果上均优于传统迭代优化方法。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data remains challenging due to the need to recover directed structure and latent confounding without interventions. We propose FoundCause, an amortized causal discovery model trained entirely on synthetic data that maps datasets directly to causal graphs in a single forward pass. By learning from large collections of simulated structural causal models, FoundCause captures transferable statistical patterns that generalize beyond individual datasets. The architecture incorporates several key inductive biases for causal discovery. It uses a permutation-invariant transformer encoder with alternating attention over samples and variables to jointly model cross-variable dependence and per-variable distributions. Pairwise statistical features derived from classical asymmetry measures are injected through statistics-conditioned attention, guiding the model toward known causal signals. A factorized decoder separates edge existence from direction, while a triangular refinement module enables reasoning over higher-order causal motifs such as chains and colliders. In addition, a dedicated confounder module based on learnable latent tokens explicitly models hidden common causes, and the model explicitly handles missing data via its masked input representation. To our knowledge, FoundCause is the first amortized causal discovery approach to explicitly model latent confounding. FoundCause outperforms 11 classical non-amortized methods (e.g., PC, GES, NOTEARS-style optimization) and 4 amortized causal discovery methods on 15 real-world datasets, achieving +9.6% improvement in $F_1$, +1.2% in AUROC, and an 18.9% reduction in structural Hamming distance relative to the strongest non-amortized methods, while performing inference in a single forward pass.

</details>


### [174] [Learning to Refine Hidden States for Reliable LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17524)
*Chia-Hsuan Hsu,Jui-Ming Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReLAR框架，通过强化学习引导的隐式潜在表示迭代精炼，在解码前自适应地更新隐藏状态，以提升多步推理的稳定性和准确性，同时降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在复杂多步推理中，早期隐藏状态错误会传播导致错误预测，内部推理过程不稳定，需要一种更稳定高效的推理机制。

Method: 提出ReLAR框架，维护紧凑的潜在推理状态，使用学习到的深度控制器和动作控制器自适应决定精炼步数和方向，通过基于步进似然提升的策略梯度目标训练控制器。

Result: 在医学、数学、多跳推理和开放生成基准上，ReLAR提升了准确性、生成质量和推理稳定性，且推理开销显著低于显式推理基线。

Conclusion: ReLAR通过强化学习引导的隐式潜在精炼，实现了高效、稳定、输入自适应的推理，无需显式思维链生成。

Abstract: Large language models show strong reasoning ability, but their internal reasoning process can remain unstable in complex multi-step settings, where early hidden-state errors may propagate to incorrect predictions. We propose ReLAR, a reinforcement-guided latent refinement framework that iteratively updates hidden representations before decoding. ReLAR maintains a compact latent reasoning state and uses learned depth and action controllers to adaptively determine both the number and direction of refinement steps. The controllers are trained with a policy gradient objective based on step-wise likelihood improvement, enabling efficient input-dependent reasoning without explicit chain-of-thought generation. Experiments on medical, mathematical, multi-hop reasoning, and open-ended generation benchmarks show that ReLAR improves accuracy, generation quality, and reasoning stability with substantially lower inference overhead than explicit reasoning baselines.

</details>


### [175] [MGUP: A Momentum-Gradient Alignment Update Policy for Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17526)
*Da Chang,Ganzhao Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MGUP机制，通过每轮迭代对固定比例的参数施加较大步长、其余施加较小步长，增强动量优化器（如AdamW、Lion、Muon），在提供理论收敛保证的同时，实现高效稳定的训练，在MAE预训练、LLM预训练和下游微调中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层内选择性更新方法缺乏细粒度控制且缺乏收敛保证，亟需一种兼具理论保障与实用性的通用选择性更新机制。

Method: MGUP在每一迭代中对选定固定比例的参数使用较大步长，其余参数使用较小非零步长；该机制作为即插即用模块，可无缝集成到AdamW、Lion、Muon等优化器中，并在标准假设下为MGUP-AdamW（无权重衰减）提供了随机优化的理论收敛保证。

Result: 在MAE预训练、LLM预训练及下游微调等多样化任务中，MGUP增强的优化器相比原始优化器取得了更优或更稳定的性能。

Conclusion: MGUP提供了一种有原则、通用且具理论基础的层内选择性更新策略，能够加速和稳定大规模模型的训练。

Abstract: Efficient optimization is essential for training large language models. Although intra-layer selective updates have been explored, a general mechanism that enables fine-grained control while ensuring convergence guarantees is still lacking. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{MGUP}, a novel mechanism for selective updates. \textbf{MGUP} augments standard momentum-based optimizers by applying larger step-sizes to a selected fixed proportion of parameters in each iteration, while applying smaller, non-zero step-sizes to the rest. As a nearly {plug-and-play} module, \textbf{MGUP} seamlessly integrates with optimizers such as AdamW, Lion, and Muon. This yields powerful variants such as \textbf{MGUP-AdamW}, \textbf{MGUP-Lion}, and \textbf{MGUP-Muon}. Under standard assumptions, we provide theoretical convergence guarantees for \textbf{MGUP-AdamW} (without weight decay) in stochastic optimization. Extensive experiments across diverse tasks, including MAE pretraining, LLM pretraining, and downstream fine-tuning, demonstrate that our \textbf{MGUP}-enhanced optimizers achieve superior or more stable performance compared to their original base optimizers. We offer a principled, versatile, and theoretically grounded strategy for efficient intra-layer selective updates, accelerating and stabilizing the training of large-scale models. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/MaeChd/MGUP.

</details>


### [176] [Non-negative Matrix Factorisation with Topological Regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17531)
*Matias de Jong van Lier,Shizuo Kaji,Keunsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在非负矩阵分解（NMF）中利用持续同调作为拓扑正则化器，学习具有可解释性的基函数，实现对空间、时间和图结构数据的统一建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多数据模态可视为结构化域上的非负函数，基函数的质量与其拓扑结构内在相关，但传统的拓扑整合方法受限于离散性和阈值依赖，无法用于连续优化。

Method: 采用持续同调作为稳定且无阈值的拓扑量化工具，设计拓扑评分并将其作为正则化项融入NMF目标函数。

Result: 该框架能够统一处理空间连贯的图像成分、周期性的时间序列结构以及团状图信号。

Conclusion: 通过持续同调解耦NMF基函数的拓扑正则化，实现了跨模态的可解释基学习，克服了传统方法的离散性和阈值依赖问题。

Abstract: We investigate the learning of interpretable bases in non-negative matrix factorisation (NMF) by regularising the topology of the learned basis functions. Our approach is motivated by the observation that many data modalities can be viewed as non-negative functions on a structured domain, where the quality of a basis is intrinsically linked to its topology. However, naive methods for incorporating the topology of the support are often hindered by discreteness and threshold dependence, rendering them unsuitable for continuous optimisation. We address these challenges by employing persistent homology as a stable, threshold-free topological quantifier and by designing topological scores that integrate into the NMF objective as regularisers. The resulting framework encompasses spatially coherent image components, periodic time-series structures, and clique-like graph signals within a unified modelling language.

</details>


### [177] [Offline Preference-Based Trajectory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17541)
*Fernando Diaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出基于偏好的轨迹评估方法，通过时间偏好比较代理系统的完整轨迹（包括进度和返回时间），在多个交互式基准测试中将比较平局率从约75%降至约35%，显著提升了区分能力、排名稳定性和数据效率。研究表明基准测试饱和可能源于评估指标选择而非数据或难度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线评估代理系统时，传统方法仅关注最终成功状态，忽略了轨迹中的部分进展信息，导致大量平局（约75%），造成统计效率低下，削弱了区分不同系统的能力。

Method: 提出偏好-based轨迹评估方法，直接比较轨迹的时间偏好（进度和时间-返回曲线），而非仅比较终端成功状态。

Result: 在多种代理和交互式基准测试中，传统成功指标产生约75%的平局，而轨迹感知偏好将平局率降至约35%，提升了区分力、排名稳定性和数据效率。

Conclusion: 基准测试饱和现象可能不仅源于数据收集或问题难度，评估指标的选择也是一个重要影响因素。

Abstract: Offline evaluation of agentic systems often collapses trajectories to terminal success, discarding information about partial progress and inducing widespread ties, creating substantial statistical inefficiency by reducing effective sample size and weakening the ability to distinguish systems. We propose preference-based trajectory evaluation, which compares trajectories directly through temporal preferences over progress and time-to-return profiles. We find that, across diverse agentic and interactive benchmarks, standard success-based metrics produce tied comparisons on roughly 75% of instances, whereas trajectory-aware preferences reduce ties to roughly 35%, improving discriminative power, ranking stability, and data efficiency. Our results suggest that benchmark saturation, often attributed to poor data collection or problem difficulty, may also be explained by the choice of evaluation measure.

</details>


### [178] [Continuous-time Optimal Stopping through Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17545)
*Cosmin Borsa,Michael Ludkovski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CARLOS算法，利用聚合深度神经网络和自适应采样策略，通过渐进式时间网格细化学习任意精细时间分辨率下的最优停止规则，在计算效率接近的情况下获得比现有Bermudan求解器更高的价格，逼近American上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优停止问题求解器受限于离散化时间网格：粗网格低估期望收益，细网格则通过反向递归累积近似误差。现有方法无法同时克服这两方面限制。

Method: 提出CARLOS算法：1) 使用聚合深度神经网络学习联合时空决策边界；2) 从粗时间网格开始，逐步增加停止机会频率，同时并行训练ADNN精化价值估计；3) 设计自适应采样策略，逐步将训练集中到停止边界附近。

Result: CARLOS比现有Bermudan求解器获得更高价格，接近American上界；同时相对于非强化学习对比方法，计算效率更高。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习的时间网格渐进细化方法有效克服了传统离散化方法在粗/细网格下的两难困境，实现了任意精细时间分辨率下最优停止规则的有效学习。

Abstract: Simulation based solvers for optimal stopping problems must discretize the stopping decision. Under classical dynamic programming, a coarse exercise grid with only a few stopping opportunities can materially undervalue the optimal expected reward, whereas on a very fine grid, approximation errors accumulate through the backward recursion. To remove this limitation, we develop a new reinforcement-learning inspired algorithm that enables us to learn the exercise rule at arbitrarily fine time resolution. Our CARLOS (Continuous-time Adaptive Reinforcement Learning for Optimal Stopping) algorithm utilizes an aggregate deep neural network (ADNN) to learn a joint space-time decision boundary. Starting from a coarse time grid, we progressively increase the frequency of stopping opportunities, while in parallel training the ADNN to refine its timing-value estimates. We moreover design an adaptive sampling strategy that gradually concentrates training effort near the stopping boundary. Benchmarked results show that CARLOS delivers higher prices than existing Bermudan solvers, approaching the American upper bound, and achieves high computational efficiency relative to non-RL comparators.

</details>


### [179] [Reversal Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17551)
*Aditya Oberai,Seohong Park,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reversal Q-learning (RQL)，一种基于流匹配的离策略强化学习算法。RQL通过扩展MDP框架将流细化步骤视为独立动作，利用流反向生成虚拟在策略轨迹，并应用偏差-方差缩减技术缓解离策略RL的horizon问题。实验证明RQL在50个机器人任务中达到了最先进的离线RL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的RL方法存在通过时间反向传播(BPTT)的问题，未能充分利用学习到的价值函数，且无法直接训练完整的、表达力强的流策略。

Method: 1) 采用"扩展"MDP框架，将流建模的每个细化步骤视为MDP中的独立动作；2) 通过反向流生成虚拟在策略轨迹，使离策略RL与先验数据兼容；3) 应用偏差-方差缩减技术缓解离策略RL中的horizon诅咒问题。

Result: 在50个具有挑战性的模拟机器人任务上，RQL相比最先进的基于流的离线RL算法，取得了最佳的平均离线RL性能。

Conclusion: RQL避免了BPTT问题，更好地利用了学习到的价值函数，并能直接训练完整表达力的流策略，在离线RL中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Iterative generative modeling techniques, such as flow matching, provide powerful tools to model complex behaviors for effective offline reinforcement learning (RL). In this work, we propose a new off-policy RL algorithm that trains a flow policy based on prior data. Our idea starts from the "expanded" Markov decision process (MDP) framework, which treats individual flow refinement steps as separate actions in an MDP. To enable off-policy RL within this framework, we apply two techniques: we generate virtual on-policy trajectories (by "reversing" flows) to make this framework compatible with prior data, and we apply a bias-and-variance reduction technique to mitigate the curse of horizon in off-policy RL. We call the resulting algorithm Reversal Q-learning (RQL). RQL has several advantages over previous flow-based RL methods: it does not suffer from backpropagation through time, makes better use of the learned value function, and directly trains the full, expressive flow policy. Through our experiments on 50 challenging simulated robotic tasks, we show that RQL leads to the best average offline RL performance compared to state-of-the-art flow-based offline RL algorithms.

</details>


### [180] [SpatioTemporal Causal Network Diagnostics for Geographic Tipping Point Early Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17553)
*Zhaoyuan Yu,Zhangyong Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ST-CND框架，通过构建时变因果网络（传递熵推断信息流拓扑、动态模态分解估计恢复率、综合内部波动/同步/外部耦合三个信号）来解决空间早期预警中的空间稀释、欧几里得假设和噪声相关问题，在合成分岔和SST基准（AMOC AUROC=0.783）上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典空间指标（如Moran's I）面临空间稀释、欧几里得假设和空间相关噪声三个问题，无法有效提供局部化的早期预警。

Method: (1) 用传递熵推断空间节点间的信息流拓扑，替代固定欧几里得邻域；(2) 用动态模态分解估计候选子网络局部恢复率；(3) 综合高内部波动、高内部同步和低外部耦合三个信号识别最脆弱子网络，抑制噪声虚假警报。

Result: 在AMOC任务上AUROC达0.783，关键子网络IoU为0.378，优于递归网络和lambda-AR1基线。

Conclusion: ST-CND提供了一种可解释、可扩展的空间早期预警流程，适用于地球系统科学。

Abstract: Geographic tipping points in ecosystems, climate subsystems, or ice sheets pose severe challenges for localized early warning. Classical spatial indicators such as Moran's I summarize global spatial structure, but they struggle with three issues: spatial dilution, Euclidean assumptions, and correlated noise. This paper introduces SpatioTemporal Causal Network Diagnostics (ST-CND), a framework that addresses these three issues by representing the geographic field as a time-evolving directed causal network. The core workflow is as follows: (1) infer which spatial nodes help predict other nodes via transfer entropy, replacing fixed Euclidean neighborhoods with data-driven information-flow topology; (2) estimate local recovery rates within each candidate subnetwork via dynamic mode decomposition; and (3) identify the most vulnerable subnetwork by combining three signals, namely high internal fluctuation, high internal synchronization, and low external coupling, thereby suppressing false alarms from spatially correlated noise. Validated on synthetic bifurcations and two observational sea-surface temperature benchmarks, namely Indo-Pacific SST and North Atlantic AMOC, ST-CND delivers localized and interpretable warnings. On the AMOC task, it achieves an AUROC of 0.783 and a critical-subnetwork IoU of 0.378, outperforming recurrence-network and lambda-AR1 baselines. The framework provides an interpretable and scalable pipeline for spatial early warning in Earth system science.

</details>


### [181] [Reducing Learner Redundancy in Boosting via Residual Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17567)
*Ye Su,Jipeng Guo,Yong Liu,Xin Xu,Gangchun Zhang,Jinxin Chen,Di Wu,Longlong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCBoost框架，通过从残差拟合转向残差正交化来消除标准Boosting中的学习器冗余。核心机制包括：训练阶段用谱残差投影(SRP)将残差目标投影到历史预测子空间的正交补上；聚合阶段用协方差正则化加权(CRW)优化集成权重。理论证明SRP产生精确的加性残差能量分解，并在各向同性噪声假设下建立信噪比改进条件。十个基准数据集的实验表明SCBoost在准确率和F1分数上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Boosting框架中顺序残差拟合会反复关联误差分量，导致学习器冗余，需要更有效的冗余控制机制。

Method: 提出SCBoost框架，包含两个互补机制：(1) 谱残差投影(SRP)——训练时将每个残差目标投影到历史预测子空间的正交补上；(2) 协方差正则化加权(CRW)——聚合时在验证集上通过显式协方差惩罚优化集成权重以缓解剩余相关性。

Result: 在十个基准数据集上，SCBoost在开箱即用场景下表现优异，尤其在准确率和F1分数指标上突出。

Conclusion: 该工作通过几何视角重新诠释了Boosting，表明显式冗余控制是实现更高效集成架构的原则性且必要的步骤。

Abstract: While sequential residual fitting is the bedrock of standard boosting frameworks, it inherently breeds learner redundancy by repeatedly revisiting correlated error components. To address this bottleneck, we propose a shift from residual fitting to \textit{residual orthogonalization} and introduce SCBoost. Our framework tackles redundancy through two complementary mechanisms: Spectral Residual Projection (SRP) and Covariance-Regularized Weighting (CRW). During training, SRP projects each residual target onto the orthogonal complement of the historical prediction subspace, forcing successive learners to capture only novel empirical innovations. During aggregation, CRW optimizes ensemble weights on a validation set with an explicit covariance penalty to mitigate remaining correlations. Theoretically, we provide a finite-sample geometric characterization proving that SRP yields an exact additive residual-energy decomposition. Furthermore, under an isotropic-noise assumption, we rigorously establish the conditions under which this projection improves the effective Signal-to-Noise Ratio. Extensive experiments across ten benchmark datasets demonstrate that SCBoost delivers strong out-of-the-box performance, particularly in accuracy and F1 score. This work reinterprets boosting through a geometric lens, suggesting that explicit redundancy control is a principled and necessary step toward more efficient ensemble architectures.

</details>


### [182] [When Dynamics Models Read the Wrong Time Steps: Label-Free Event Credit Re-Anchoring for Robust Global Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17572)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"时序信用稀释"问题：轨迹级监督下，模型会依赖平滑相关信号而非关键物理事件进行预测。为此提出Credit-in-Event探针和CREST方法（免训练、免标签），通过事件核心对比重锚定来恢复事件信用，在齿轮/冲击系统等任务上有效降低分布外误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习动力学模型使用"序列到全局"的接口（将每一步特征池化为一个读出头向量），但这种设计存在时序信用分配问题：仅有轨迹级监督时，模型可以利用平滑的相关特征（而非决定目标的短暂物理事件）来准确预测，作者称此现象为"时序信用稀释"（temporal credit dilution），且训练损失无法暴露此问题，标准物理约束残差也无法消除。

Method: 1) 提出Credit-in-Event探针：测量全局池化信用有多少落在事件步骤上，并闭式证明在事件占比缩小时线性池化读出器会将信用路由到虚假背景通道；2) 提出CREST：一种免训练、免标签的读出头，从学习到的特征中估计瞬态事件核心，通过事件-背景对比（event-versus-rest contrast）重锚定池化表示。

Result: 在模拟齿轮系统、冲击系统、循环/注意力编码器以及公开轴承振动数据上，CREST降低了分布外误差并恢复了事件信用。消融实验表明，稳定步骤选择和感受野收缩策略均失败，证实收益来自事件核心信用重锚定而非通用局部性或稳定性先验。

Conclusion: 时序信用稀释是动力学模型学习中的关键问题，CREST提供了一种有效的免训练解决方案，通过事件核心的对比重锚定来纠正信用分配，优于传统的局部性或稳定性先验方法。

Abstract: Learned dynamics models often answer global physical questions, such as fault severity or impact stiffness, by pooling a per-step feature sequence into one readout vector. This sequence-to-global interface creates an under-studied temporal credit problem: with only trajectory-level supervision, a model can predict accurately in training conditions while reading from abundant smooth correlates rather than the brief physical events that determine the target. We call this failure temporal credit dilution. It is not exposed by the training loss and is not removed by standard physics-informed residuals, because the error lies in where the global readout assigns functional credit. We introduce Credit-in-Event, an interface-level probe for measuring how much pooled credit lands on event steps, and prove in closed form that a pooled linear reader routes credit to a spurious background channel as the event fraction shrinks. We then propose CREST, a training-free and label-free readout that estimates a transient event core from learned features and re-anchors the pooled representation through event-versus-rest contrast. Across simulated gear and impact systems, recurrent and attention encoders, and public bearing vibration data, CREST reduces out-of-distribution error while restoring event credit. Ablations show that stable-step selection and receptive-field shrinking fail, confirming that the gain comes from event-core credit re-anchoring rather than a generic locality or stability prior.

</details>


### [183] [LLM Features Can Hurt GNNs: Concatenation Interference on Homophilous Graph Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17579)
*Zhongyuan Wang,Pratyusha Vemuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，将LLM生成的节点特征通过纯输入拼接（而非联合训练、蒸馏或提示条件化）引入GNN时，会在同质性基准上系统性地降低准确率。使用MLP骨干、Planetoid公共分割和词袋原始特征，PubMed测试准确率下降17.0±0.3个百分点，Cora下降4.3±0.6个百分点。这种下降在更强大的骨干网络、随机分割和更小编码器下减弱，在中同质性数据集上逆转。作者提出LLM单独可判别性指标Delta_sig来预测拼接成本，并建立了幂律关系。

论文标题推断：LLM Features in GNNs: When Concatenation Hurts


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛报道将LLM生成的特征加入GNN可提升准确率，但本文观察到相反现象：通过纯输入拼接方式引入LLM特征时，会在同质性基准上系统性地降低准确率。作者希望理解这一矛盾现象的成因，并找出何时拼接有帮助、何时有害的预测指标。

Method: 使用MLP骨干网络、Planetoid公共数据分割和词袋原始特征，将SBERT编码的GPT-4o-mini TAPE特征通过纯输入拼接引入。系统性地改变骨干网络（GCN/GCNII/GAT）、数据分割方式、编码器大小等条件。提出Delta_sig（LLM单独可判别性指标）来预测拼接效果。进行维度控制消融实验和幂律拟合分析。

Result: PubMed测试准确率下降17.0±0.3个百分点，Cora下降4.3±0.6个百分点，CiteSeer下降0.6±0.8个百分点（在种子噪声范围内）。使用更强的骨干网络、随机分割、更小的编码器时下降减弱。WikiCS提升4.4个百分点，ogbn-arxiv提升11.7个百分点。Delta_sig与拼接成本的相关系数r²=0.38，优于同质性指标（r²=0.06）。9个数据集中的7个被Delta_sig阈值规则正确分类。幂律拟合r²=0.97。

Conclusion: 纯输入拼接方式引入LLM特征并不总是有益的，其效果取决于LLM特征自身的判别性（Delta_sig）以及数据集的规模。LLM特征在高判别性、大规模数据集上有帮助，在低判别性、小规模数据集上有害。Delta_sig可作为解释性视角而非精确过滤器来使用。

Abstract: Adding LLM-generated node features to graph neural networks (GNNs) is widely reported to improve accuracy on standard benchmarks. We document a contrasting observation: when LLM features are introduced through pure input concatenation (rather than joint training, distillation, or prompt-conditioning), they can systematically degrade accuracy on the same homophilous benchmarks where end-to-end LLM pipelines succeed. With an MLP backbone on the Planetoid public split and bag-of-words original features, concatenating SBERT-encoded GPT-4o-mini TAPE features reduces PubMed test accuracy by -17.0 +/- 0.3 pp and Cora by -4.3 +/- 0.6 pp (CiteSeer -0.6 +/- 0.8 pp, within seed noise). The drop attenuates as we relax each condition (GCN / GCNII / GAT backbones, random splits, smaller encoders) and reverses on medium-homophily WikiCS (+4.4 pp) and ogbn-arxiv (+11.7 pp). To predict when concatenation helps versus hurts, we report a simple measure of LLM-alone discriminability, Delta_sig. Across 9 datasets Delta_sig correlates with the concatenation cost more strongly than homophily at point estimate (r^2 = 0.38 vs. 0.06; N=9, bootstrap CIs overlap). The bootstrap-best change-point is tau = 13.8 pp, and the rule "Delta_sig <= tau predicts non-positive concat cost" classifies 7/9 datasets correctly; since 60% of bootstrap samples place tau in [5, 30] pp, we treat Delta_sig as an interpretive lens rather than a precision filter. A dimension-controlled ablation on PubMed places the LLM-feature drop between same-source PCA (-2.3 pp) and same-dim Gaussian noise (-37.3 pp), ruling out dimensionality and weight-decay artifacts.
